File: dumb.js
================
const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

const outputFile = path.join(__dirname, "project_code.txt");
const allowedExtensions = new Set([
  ".ts",
  ".tsx",
  ".js",
  ".jsx",
  ".json",
  ".css",
  ".mjs",
]);
const excludedFiles = new Set([
  "package-lock.json",
  "yarn.lock",
  "pnpm-lock.yaml",
  "dump.js",
  "project_code.txt",
]);

let combinedContent = "";

function scan(targetPath) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(targetPath)) return;
  const stat = fs.statSync(targetPath);

  if (stat.isDirectory()) {
    // Hard block any node_modules, build caches, or git folders
    const baseName = path.basename(targetPath);
    if (
      [
        "node_modules",
        ".next",
        ".git",
        ".vscode",
        "public",
        "dist",
        "build",
        "coverage",
      ].includes(baseName)
    ) {
      return;
    }
    const entries = fs.readdirSync(targetPath);
    for (const entry of entries) {
      scan(path.join(targetPath, entry));
    }
  } else if (stat.isFile()) {
    const ext = path.extname(targetPath);
    const fileName = path.basename(targetPath);

    if (allowedExtensions.has(ext) && !excludedFiles.has(fileName)) {
      const relPath = path.relative(__dirname, targetPath);
      const content = fs.readFileSync(targetPath, "utf-8");
      combinedContent += `\n==============================================================\nFILE: ${relPath}\n==============================================================\n\n${content}\n\n`;
    }
  }
}

// 1. Only scan the src folder
scan(path.join(__dirname, "src"));

// 2. Only grab explicit root configs
const rootConfigs = [
  "package.json",
  "next.config.mjs",
  "next.config.js",
  "tailwind.config.ts",
  "tailwind.config.js",
  "tsconfig.json",
];

for (const config of rootConfigs) {
  const fullConfigPath = path.join(__dirname, config);
  if (fs.existsSync(fullConfigPath)) {
    const content = fs.readFileSync(fullConfigPath, "utf-8");
    combinedContent += `\n==============================================================\nFILE: ${config}\n==============================================================\n\n${content}\n\n`;
  }
}

fs.writeFileSync(outputFile, combinedContent, "utf-8");
const stats = fs.statSync(outputFile);
console.log(
  `Done! File size: ${(stats.size / 1024).toFixed(2)} KB -> ${outputFile}`,
);

This is a [Next.js](https://nextjs.org) project bootstrapped with [`create-next-app`](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/api-reference/cli/create-next-app).

## Getting Started

First, run the development server:

```bash
npm run dev
# or
yarn dev
# or
pnpm dev
# or
bun dev
```

Open [http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000) with your browser to see the result.

You can start editing the page by modifying `app/page.tsx`. The page auto-updates as you edit the file.

This project uses [`next/font`](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/optimizing/fonts) to automatically optimize and load [Geist](https://vercel.com/font), a new font family for Vercel.

## Learn More

To learn more about Next.js, take a look at the following resources:

- [Next.js Documentation](https://nextjs.org/docs) - learn about Next.js features and API.
- [Learn Next.js](https://nextjs.org/learn) - an interactive Next.js tutorial.

You can check out [the Next.js GitHub repository](https://github.com/vercel/next.js) - your feedback and contributions are welcome!

File: src/app/account/addresses/page.tsx
================
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useSession } from "next-auth/react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { ArrowLeft, Check, MapPin, Plus, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";

export const INDIAN_STATES = [
  "Andaman and Nicobar Islands",
  "Andhra Pradesh",
  "Arunachal Pradesh",
  "Assam",
  "Bihar",
  "Chandigarh",
  "Chhattisgarh",
  "Dadra and Nagar Haveli and Daman and Diu",
  "Delhi",
  "Goa",
  "Gujarat",
  "Haryana",
  "Himachal Pradesh",
  "Jammu and Kashmir",
  "Jharkhand",
  "Karnataka",
  "Kerala",
  "Ladakh",
  "Lakshadweep",
  "Madhya Pradesh",
  "Maharashtra",
  "Manipur",
  "Meghalaya",
  "Mizoram",
  "Nagaland",
  "Odisha",
  "Puducherry",
  "Punjab",
  "Rajasthan",
  "Sikkim",
  "Tamil Nadu",
  "Telangana",
  "Tripura",
  "Uttar Pradesh",
  "Uttarakhand",
  "West Bengal",
] as const;

interface Address {
  _id: string;
  type: "HOME" | "WORK" | "OTHER";
  fullName: string;
  phone: string;
  address: string;
  city: string;
  state: string;
  pincode: string;
  isDefault: boolean;
}

const emptyForm = {
  fullName: "",
  phone: "",
  address: "",
  city: "",
  state: "Delhi",
  pincode: "",
  type: "HOME" as "HOME" | "WORK" | "OTHER",
  isDefault: false,
};

function isValidIndianPincode(pin: string): boolean {
  const clean = pin.replace(/\D/g, "");
  return clean.length === 6 && /^[1-9][0-9]{5}$/.test(clean);
}

function isValidIndianPhone(phone: string): boolean {
  const digits = phone.replace(/\D/g, "");
  return digits.length >= 10 && /^[6-9]\d{9}$/.test(digits.slice(-10));
}

export default function AddressesPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { data: session, status } = useSession();

  const [addresses, setAddresses] = useState<Address[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [showAddModal, setShowAddModal] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState(emptyForm);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === "unauthenticated") {
      router.push("/account/login?callbackUrl=/account/addresses");
    }
  }, [status, router]);

  const loadAddresses = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/account/addresses");
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok && data.addresses) {
        setAddresses(data.addresses);
      }
    } catch {
      toast.error("Failed to load saved addresses");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status !== "authenticated") return;

    let isMounted = true;
    async function fetchData() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/account/addresses");
        const data = await res.json();
        if (isMounted && res.ok && data.addresses) {
          setAddresses(data.addresses);
        }
      } catch {
        if (isMounted) toast.error("Failed to load saved addresses");
      } finally {
        if (isMounted) setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    fetchData();
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [status]);

  const handleCreate = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();

    const cleanPin = form.pincode.replace(/\D/g, "");
    const cleanPhone = form.phone.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(-10);

    if (!isValidIndianPincode(cleanPin)) {
      toast.error("Please enter a valid 6-digit Indian PIN code (e.g. 110025)");
      return;
    }

    if (!isValidIndianPhone(cleanPhone)) {
      toast.error("Please enter a valid 10-digit Indian mobile number");
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/account/addresses", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ...form,
          phone: cleanPhone,
          pincode: cleanPin,
        }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error || "Failed to save address");

      toast.success("Address added successfully");
      setShowAddModal(false);
      setForm(emptyForm);
      await loadAddresses();
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Error saving address";
      toast.error(msg);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (id: string) => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/account/addresses?id=${id}`, {
        method: "DELETE",
      });

      if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Failed to delete address");

      toast.success("Address removed");
      setAddresses((prev) => prev.filter((a) => a._id !== id));
    } catch {
      toast.error("Failed to delete address");
    }
  };

  if (status === "loading" || loading) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[60vh] max-w-5xl items-center justify-center px-4">
        <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.3em] text-muted-foreground animate-pulse">
          Loading addresses...
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 py-12 space-y-8">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border pb-6">
        <div>
          <Link
            href="/account"
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground transition mb-2 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Back to Account
          </Link>
          <h1 className="font-display text-3xl tracking-wide uppercase">
            Saved Addresses
          </h1>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setShowAddModal(true)}
          className="cursor-pointer bg-brand text-brand-foreground px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold tracking-widest uppercase flex items-center gap-1.5 hover:opacity-90 transition"
        >
          <Plus className="w-4 h-4" /> Add New
        </button>
      </div>

      {addresses.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="border border-border p-12 text-center bg-card rounded-lg">
          <MapPin className="w-8 h-8 mx-auto text-muted-foreground mb-3" />
          <p className="text-sm tracking-wider uppercase font-semibold">
            No saved addresses yet
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">
            Add your primary shipping destination for 1-click checkout.
          </p>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setShowAddModal(true)}
            className="cursor-pointer bg-foreground text-background px-6 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold tracking-widest uppercase mt-5 hover:opacity-90 transition"
          >
            Add Address
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          {addresses.map((addr) => (
            <div
              key={addr._id}
              className={`p-5 border rounded-lg bg-card space-y-2 relative transition ${
                addr.isDefault
                  ? "border-brand ring-1 ring-brand"
                  : "border-border"
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold tracking-widest uppercase bg-ink text-background px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                  {addr.type}
                </span>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  {addr.isDefault && (
                    <span className="text-brand flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-bold tracking-wider uppercase">
                      <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Default
                    </span>
                  )}
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleDelete(addr._id)}
                    className="cursor-pointer text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive p-1 transition"
                    aria-label="Delete address"
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
                {addr.fullName}
              </p>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{addr.address}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                {addr.city}, {addr.state} — {addr.pincode}
              </p>
              <p className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground pt-1">
                +91 {addr.phone}
              </p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Add Address Modal with State Dropdown and Reliable PIN Validation */}
      {showAddModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/60 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-card border border-border p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-lg max-w-lg w-full space-y-5">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border pb-3">
              <h2 className="text-xl font-display uppercase tracking-wider">
                Add New Address
              </h2>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowAddModal(false)}
                className="cursor-pointer text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground text-xs uppercase tracking-widest"
              >
                Cancel
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleCreate} className="space-y-4">
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                {(["HOME", "WORK", "OTHER"] as const).map((t) => (
                  <button
                    key={t}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setForm({ ...form, type: t })}
                    className={`cursor-pointer px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold tracking-widest uppercase border transition ${
                      form.type === t
                        ? "bg-brand text-brand-foreground border-brand"
                        : "border-border text-muted-foreground hover:border-foreground"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {t}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground block mb-1">
                  Full Name
                </label>
                <input
                  required
                  type="text"
                  value={form.fullName}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setForm({ ...form, fullName: e.target.value })
                  }
                  className="w-full border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm rounded outline-none focus:border-foreground"
                  placeholder="Receiver's name"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground block mb-1">
                  Mobile Number (10 digits)
                </label>
                <input
                  required
                  type="tel"
                  maxLength={10}
                  value={form.phone}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setForm({
                      ...form,
                      phone: e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 10),
                    })
                  }
                  className="w-full border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm rounded outline-none focus:border-foreground"
                  placeholder="7982846176"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground block mb-1">
                  Street Address / Flat / Floor
                </label>
                <input
                  required
                  type="text"
                  value={form.address}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setForm({ ...form, address: e.target.value })
                  }
                  className="w-full border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm rounded outline-none focus:border-foreground"
                  placeholder="House no., Building name, Street"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground block mb-1">
                    City
                  </label>
                  <input
                    required
                    type="text"
                    value={form.city}
                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, city: e.target.value })}
                    className="w-full border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm rounded outline-none focus:border-foreground"
                    placeholder="New Delhi"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground block mb-1">
                    State
                  </label>
                  <select
                    required
                    value={form.state}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setForm({ ...form, state: e.target.value })
                    }
                    className="w-full border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm rounded outline-none focus:border-foreground cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    {INDIAN_STATES.map((st) => (
                      <option key={st} value={st}>
                        {st}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground block mb-1">
                  PIN Code (6 digits)
                </label>
                <input
                  required
                  type="text"
                  maxLength={6}
                  value={form.pincode}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setForm({
                      ...form,
                      pincode: e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 6),
                    })
                  }
                  className="w-full border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm rounded outline-none focus:border-foreground"
                  placeholder="110025"
                />
              </div>

              <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer pt-1">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={form.isDefault}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setForm({ ...form, isDefault: e.target.checked })
                  }
                  className="size-4 accent-brand cursor-pointer"
                />
                <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">
                  Make this my default delivery address
                </span>
              </label>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={saving}
                className="w-full bg-brand text-brand-foreground py-3 text-xs font-bold tracking-widest uppercase cursor-pointer hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-50 transition"
              >
                {saving ? "Saving..." : "Save Address"}
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState, Suspense } from "react";
import { signIn } from "next-auth/react";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { toast } from "sonner";

function LoginForm() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const callbackUrl = searchParams.get("callbackUrl") || "/account";

  const [isRegister, setIsRegister] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [googleLoading, setGoogleLoading] = useState(false);

  const [form, setForm] = useState({ name: "", email: "", password: "" });

  const handleGoogleSignIn = async () => {
    try {
      setGoogleLoading(true);
      await signIn("google", { callbackUrl });
    } catch {
      toast.error("Failed to connect with Google.");
      setGoogleLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      if (isRegister) {
        const res = await fetch("/api/auth/register", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify(form),
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error);

        toast.success("Account created! Signing you in...");
      }

      const loginRes = await signIn("credentials", {
        redirect: false,
        email: form.email,
        password: form.password,
      });

      if (loginRes?.error) {
        toast.error("Invalid email or password.");
      } else {
        router.push(callbackUrl);
        router.refresh();
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const message =
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Something went wrong.";
      toast.error(message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-md space-y-6 border border-border bg-card p-8 rounded-lg shadow-sm">
      <div className="text-center space-y-2">
        <h1 className="font-display text-3xl tracking-wider uppercase">
          {isRegister ? "Join CONSCENT" : "Welcome Back"}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-xs tracking-wider text-muted-foreground uppercase">
          {isRegister
            ? "Create an account to track orders & view history"
            : "Sign in to access your orders and profile"}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Google One-Click Login Button */}
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled={googleLoading || loading}
        onClick={handleGoogleSignIn}
        className="cursor-pointer w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-3 border border-border bg-background py-2.5 px-4 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wider uppercase rounded hover:bg-neutral-50 dark:hover:bg-neutral-900 transition disabled:opacity-50"
      >
        <svg className="w-4 h-4" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path
            fill="#4285F4"
            d="M22.56 12.25c0-.78-.07-1.53-.2-2.25H12v4.26h5.92c-.26 1.37-1.04 2.53-2.21 3.31v2.77h3.57c2.08-1.92 3.28-4.74 3.28-8.09z"
          />
          <path
            fill="#34A853"
            d="M12 23c2.97 0 5.46-.98 7.28-2.66l-3.57-2.77c-.98.66-2.23 1.06-3.71 1.06-2.86 0-5.29-1.93-6.16-4.53H2.18v2.84C3.99 20.53 7.7 23 12 23z"
          />
          <path
            fill="#FBBC05"
            d="M5.84 14.09c-.22-.66-.35-1.36-.35-2.09s.13-1.43.35-2.09V7.06H2.18C1.43 8.55 1 10.22 1 12s.43 3.45 1.18 4.94l2.85-2.22.81-.63z"
          />
          <path
            fill="#EA4335"
            d="M12 5.38c1.62 0 3.06.56 4.21 1.64l3.15-3.15C17.45 2.09 14.97 1 12 1 7.7 1 3.99 3.47 2.18 7.06l3.66 2.84c.87-2.6 3.3-4.52 6.16-4.52z"
          />
        </svg>
        {googleLoading ? "Connecting..." : "Continue with Google"}
      </button>

      {/* Visual Divider */}
      <div className="relative flex items-center justify-center">
        <div className="border-t border-border w-full" />
        <span className="bg-card px-3 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground absolute">
          or
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* Credentials Form */}
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
        {isRegister && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground block mb-1">
              Full Name
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={form.name}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })}
              className="w-full border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm rounded outline-none focus:border-foreground"
              placeholder="Your Name"
            />
          </div>
        )}

        <div>
          <label className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground block mb-1">
            Email Address
          </label>
          <input
            type="email"
            required
            value={form.email}
            onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, email: e.target.value })}
            className="w-full border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm rounded outline-none focus:border-foreground"
            placeholder="you@domain.com"
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground block mb-1">
            Password
          </label>
          <input
            type="password"
            required
            value={form.password}
            onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, password: e.target.value })}
            className="w-full border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm rounded outline-none focus:border-foreground"
            placeholder="••••••••"
          />
        </div>

        <button
          type="submit"
          disabled={loading || googleLoading}
          className="w-full bg-foreground text-background py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold tracking-widest uppercase rounded hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-50 transition"
        >
          {loading
            ? "Processing..."
            : isRegister
              ? "Create Account"
              : "Sign In"}
        </button>
      </form>

      <div className="text-center pt-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setIsRegister(!isRegister)}
          className="text-xs text-muted-foreground underline hover:text-foreground"
        >
          {isRegister
            ? "Already have an account? Sign in"
            : "New to CONSCENT? Create your account"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function LoginPage() {
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-[70vh] items-center justify-center px-4 py-12">
      <Suspense
        fallback={
          <div className="text-xs tracking-widest uppercase">Loading...</div>
        }
      >
        <LoginForm />
      </Suspense>
    </div>
  );
}

import { auth } from "@/auth";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import clientPromise from "@/lib/mongodb";
import { ArrowLeft, Package, Clock, CheckCircle2, Truck } from "lucide-react";

interface OrderItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  size: string;
  quantity: number;
  price: number;
}

interface Order {
  _id: string;
  createdAt: string;
  totalAmount: number;
  status: "PENDING" | "PROCESSING" | "SHIPPED" | "DELIVERED";
  razorpayPaymentId?: string;
  items: OrderItem[];
}

export default async function OrdersPage() {
  const session = await auth();

  if (!session?.user?.id) {
    redirect("/account/login");
  }

  const client = await clientPromise;
  const db = client.db();

  // Fetch orders matching this specific user
  const rawOrders = await db
    .collection("orders")
    .find({ userId: session.user.id })
    .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
    .toArray();

  const orders: Order[] = rawOrders.map((doc) => ({
    _id: doc._id.toString(),
    createdAt: new Date(doc.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("en-IN", {
      day: "numeric",
      month: "short",
      year: "numeric",
    }),
    totalAmount: doc.totalAmount,
    status: doc.status || "PROCESSING",
    razorpayPaymentId: doc.razorpayPaymentId || "COD / Prepaid",
    items: doc.items || [],
  }));

  const getStatusBadge = (status: Order["status"]) => {
    switch (status) {
      case "DELIVERED":
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-emerald-600 bg-emerald-500/10 px-2.5 py-1 rounded">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Delivered
          </span>
        );
      case "SHIPPED":
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-blue-600 bg-blue-500/10 px-2.5 py-1 rounded">
            <Truck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> In Transit
          </span>
        );
      default:
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-amber-600 bg-amber-500/10 px-2.5 py-1 rounded">
            <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Processing Formulation
          </span>
        );
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 py-10 space-y-8">
      <div>
        <Link
          href="/account"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground mb-4 transition"
        >
          <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Back to Account
        </Link>
        <h1 className="font-display text-3xl tracking-wide uppercase">
          Your Fragrance Orders
        </h1>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground tracking-wider uppercase mt-1">
          Review batch formulations, dispatch status, and purchase invoices
        </p>
      </div>

      {orders.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-16 border border-dashed border-border rounded-lg space-y-4">
          <Package className="w-10 h-10 mx-auto text-muted-foreground/60" />
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <p className="text-base font-medium">No past orders yet</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              You haven't placed any orders with CONSCENT yet.
            </p>
          </div>
          <Link
            href="/parfums"
            className="inline-block bg-foreground text-background px-6 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest rounded hover:opacity-90 transition mt-2"
          >
            Explore Extraits
          </Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-6">
          {orders.map((order) => (
            <div
              key={order._id}
              className="border border-border bg-card rounded-lg overflow-hidden shadow-sm"
            >
              {/* Top Meta Bar */}
              <div className="bg-muted/40 px-6 py-3.5 border-b border-border flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 text-xs">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-muted-foreground block text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider">
                      Order Placed
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-medium text-foreground">
                      {order.createdAt}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-muted-foreground block text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider">
                      Total
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-medium text-foreground">
                      ₹{order.totalAmount.toLocaleString("en-IN")}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-muted-foreground block text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider">
                      Order Ref
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-mono text-muted-foreground">
                      #{order._id.slice(-8).toUpperCase()}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div>{getStatusBadge(order.status)}</div>
              </div>

              {/* Items List */}
              <div className="p-6 divide-y divide-border">
                {order.items.map((item, idx) => (
                  <div
                    key={idx}
                    className="py-4 first:pt-0 last:pb-0 flex items-center justify-between"
                  >
                    <div>
                      <h3 className="font-medium text-sm text-foreground">
                        {item.name}
                      </h3>
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">
                        Bottle Size: {item.size} • Qty: {item.quantity}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-right">
                      <span className="font-medium text-sm">
                        ₹{(item.price * item.quantity).toLocaleString("en-IN")}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

              {/* Footer */}
              <div className="bg-background px-6 py-3 border-t border-border flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
                <span>Payment: {order.razorpayPaymentId}</span>
                <span className="font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">
                  Handcrafted Artisan Batch
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ArrowLeft, ExternalLink, Package } from "lucide-react";
import { formatINR } from "@/data/products";

interface OrderItem {
  productId?: string;
  name: string;
  size: string;
  qty: number;
}

interface OrderRecord {
  _id: string;
  orderId: string;
  createdAt: string;
  total: number;
  paymentStatus: string;
  fulfillmentStatus?: string;
  awb?: string;
  items: OrderItem[];
}

export default function AccountOrdersPage() {
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<OrderRecord[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;

    async function loadAccountOrders() {
      try {
        const localSaved: string[] = JSON.parse(
          localStorage.getItem("my_orders") || "[]",
        );

        const res = await fetch("/api/account/orders", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ localOrderIds: localSaved }),
        });

        const data = await res.json();
        if (isMounted && res.ok && data.orders) {
          setOrders(data.orders);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Failed to load orders:", err);
      } finally {
        if (isMounted) setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    loadAccountOrders();

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, []);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[60vh] max-w-5xl items-center justify-center px-4">
        <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.3em] text-muted-foreground animate-pulse">
          Loading orders...
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 py-12 space-y-8">
      <div className="border-b border-border pb-6">
        <Link
          href="/account"
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground transition mb-2 cursor-pointer"
        >
          <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Back to Account
        </Link>
        <h1 className="font-display text-3xl tracking-wide uppercase">
          Your Orders
        </h1>
        <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground mt-1">
          Review batch formulations, dispatch status, and delivery tracking
        </p>
      </div>

      {orders.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="border border-border p-12 text-center bg-card rounded-lg">
          <Package className="w-8 h-8 mx-auto text-muted-foreground mb-3" />
          <p className="text-sm tracking-wider uppercase font-semibold">
            No past orders yet
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">
            You haven&apos;t placed any orders with CONSCENT yet.
          </p>
          <Link
            href="/parfums"
            className="inline-block bg-brand text-brand-foreground px-6 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold tracking-widest uppercase mt-5 hover:opacity-90 transition cursor-pointer"
          >
            Explore Extraits
          </Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {orders.map((ord) => (
            <div
              key={ord.orderId || ord._id}
              className="border border-border bg-card p-6 rounded-lg space-y-4"
            >
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-border pb-4">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold tracking-widest uppercase text-muted-foreground block">
                    Order ID
                  </span>
                  <p className="font-mono text-sm font-bold">{ord.orderId}</p>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold tracking-widest uppercase text-muted-foreground block">
                    Date Placed
                  </span>
                  <p className="text-xs">
                    {new Date(ord.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("en-IN", {
                      day: "numeric",
                      month: "short",
                      year: "numeric",
                    })}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold tracking-widest uppercase text-muted-foreground block">
                    Total
                  </span>
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold">{formatINR(ord.total)}</p>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold tracking-widest uppercase text-muted-foreground block">
                    Status
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-brand">
                    {ord.fulfillmentStatus || "ORDER PLACED"}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-2">
                {ord.items.map((it, idx) => (
                  <div
                    key={idx}
                    className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs"
                  >
                    <div>
                      <span className="font-bold uppercase tracking-wide">
                        {it.name}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-muted-foreground ml-2">
                        ({it.size} × {it.qty})
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

              {ord.awb && (
                <div className="pt-2 flex justify-end">
                  <a
                    href={`https://www.delhivery.com/track/package/${ord.awb}`}
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noreferrer"
                    className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold tracking-wider uppercase text-brand hover:underline"
                  >
                    Track Waybill: {ord.awb}{" "}
                    <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
                  </a>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

================
File: src/app/admin/page.tsx

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { formatINR } from "@/data/products";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Truck, ExternalLink, RefreshCw } from "lucide-react";

interface OrderItem {
  productId: string;
  name: string;
  size: string;
  qty: number;
  price: number;
}

interface CustomerDetails {
  fullName: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  address: string;
  city: string;
  state: string;
  pincode: string;
}

interface AdminOrder {
  orderId: string;
  customer: CustomerDetails;
  items: OrderItem[];
  subtotal: number;
  shipping: number;
  total: number;
  paymentStatus: "PENDING" | "PAID" | "FAILED";
  shippingStatus: "ORDER_PLACED" | "PACKED" | "IN_TRANSIT" | "DELIVERED";
  trackingNumber?: string;
  carrier: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export default function AdminPage() {
  const [secret, setSecret] = useState("");
  const [authenticated, setAuthenticated] = useState(false);
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<AdminOrder[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [awbInputs, setAwbInputs] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

  const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(
        `/api/admin/orders?adminSecret=${encodeURIComponent(secret)}`,
      );
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error || "Authentication failed");

      const fetchedOrders: AdminOrder[] = data.orders || [];
      setOrders(fetchedOrders);
      setAuthenticated(true);

      // Populate AWB inputs with existing tracking numbers
      const initialAwbs: Record<string, string> = {};
      fetchedOrders.forEach((o) => {
        initialAwbs[o.orderId] = o.trackingNumber || "";
      });
      setAwbInputs(initialAwbs);
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to sign in";
      toast.error(message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const updateStatus = async (
    orderId: string,
    status: AdminOrder["shippingStatus"],
    customAwb?: string,
  ) => {
    try {
      const trackingNumber =
        customAwb !== undefined ? customAwb : awbInputs[orderId];
      const res = await fetch("/api/track", {
        method: "PATCH",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          orderId,
          shippingStatus: status,
          trackingNumber,
          adminSecret: secret,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error || "Failed to update order");

      setOrders((prev) =>
        prev.map((o) =>
          o.orderId === orderId
            ? {
                ...o,
                shippingStatus: status,
                trackingNumber: trackingNumber || o.trackingNumber,
              }
            : o,
        ),
      );
      toast.success(`Updated ${orderId} -> ${status}`);
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Update failed";
      toast.error(message);
    }
  };

  const generateDemoDelhiveryAWB = (orderId: string) => {
    const mockDelhiveryWaybill = `14${Math.floor(10000000000 + Math.random() * 90000000000)}`;
    setAwbInputs((prev) => ({ ...prev, [orderId]: mockDelhiveryWaybill }));
    updateStatus(orderId, "IN_TRANSIT", mockDelhiveryWaybill);
    toast.success(`Simulated Delhivery Surface AWB: ${mockDelhiveryWaybill}`);
  };

  if (!authenticated) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-md px-4 py-24">
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
          Admin Login
        </h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs uppercase tracking-widest mt-2">
          Store Fulfillment Dashboard
        </p>
        <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="mt-6 space-y-4">
          <input
            type="password"
            placeholder="Enter ADMIN_SECRET password"
            value={secret}
            onChange={(e) => setSecret(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full border border-border bg-transparent px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-brand"
          />
          <button className="bg-brand text-brand-foreground w-full py-3 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest hover:opacity-90 transition-opacity">
            {loading ? "Checking…" : "Access Dashboard"}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1400px] px-4 py-12 sm:px-6">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-between items-center mb-8 border-b border-border pb-4 gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-5xl font-bold uppercase">Orders Dashboard</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs uppercase tracking-widest mt-1">
            Manage shipments, update AWBs, and change transit stages
          </p>
        </div>
        <span className="text-xs font-bold tracking-widest uppercase bg-tile px-4 py-2 border border-border">
          {orders.length} Total Orders
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="divide-y divide-border border border-border">
        {orders.map((o) => (
          <div
            key={o.orderId}
            className="p-6 flex flex-col xl:flex-row justify-between gap-6"
          >
            <div className="space-y-2 max-w-xl">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                <span className="font-bold text-lg">{o.orderId}</span>
                <span
                  className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 uppercase ${
                    o.paymentStatus === "PAID"
                      ? "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-500 border border-emerald-500/20"
                      : "bg-brand/10 text-brand border border-brand/20"
                  }`}
                >
                  {o.paymentStatus}
                </span>
                <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground uppercase font-mono">
                  {new Date(o.createdAt).toLocaleString("en-IN", {
                    month: "short",
                    day: "numeric",
                    hour: "2-digit",
                    minute: "2-digit",
                  })}
                </span>
              </div>

              <p className="text-sm font-semibold">
                {o.customer.fullName} · {o.customer.phone} ·{" "}
                <span className="text-muted-foreground font-normal">
                  {o.customer.email}
                </span>
              </p>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                {o.customer.address}, {o.customer.city}, {o.customer.state} -{" "}
                {o.customer.pincode}
              </p>

              {/* Order Items */}
              <div className="pt-2 text-xs space-y-1">
                {o.items.map((it, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="text-muted-foreground">
                    <span className="font-bold text-foreground uppercase">
                      {it.name}
                    </span>{" "}
                    ({it.size}) × {it.qty}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-brand pt-1">
                {formatINR(o.total)}
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* AWB Management & Stage Transitions */}
            <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 xl:w-[480px]">
              <div className="border border-border p-3 bg-tile/40">
                <label className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground block mb-2">
                  Air Waybill (Delhivery Surface AWB)
                </label>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="Enter Delhivery AWB..."
                    value={awbInputs[o.orderId] || ""}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setAwbInputs({
                        ...awbInputs,
                        [o.orderId]: e.target.value,
                      })
                    }
                    className="border border-border bg-background px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-mono flex-1 outline-none focus:border-brand"
                  />
                  <button
                    onClick={() => updateStatus(o.orderId, o.shippingStatus)}
                    className="bg-ink text-background hover:bg-brand px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    Save AWB
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => generateDemoDelhiveryAWB(o.orderId)}
                    title="Generate a realistic Delhivery tracking barcode for demo"
                    className="border border-border hover:border-brand px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <RefreshCw className="size-3" /> Demo
                  </button>
                </div>

                {o.trackingNumber && (
                  <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
                    <Truck className="size-3.5 text-brand" />
                    <span>Active Tracking:</span>
                    <a
                      href={`https://www.delhivery.com/track/package/${o.trackingNumber}`}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noreferrer"
                      className="text-brand font-mono font-bold hover:underline inline-flex items-center gap-1"
                    >
                      {o.trackingNumber}
                      <ExternalLink className="size-3" />
                    </a>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Status Buttons */}
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
                {(
                  ["ORDER_PLACED", "PACKED", "IN_TRANSIT", "DELIVERED"] as const
                ).map((status) => (
                  <button
                    key={status}
                    onClick={() => updateStatus(o.orderId, status)}
                    className={`px-3 py-2 text-[10px] font-bold tracking-widest uppercase border transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                      o.shippingStatus === status
                        ? "bg-brand text-brand-foreground border-brand"
                        : "border-border hover:border-foreground"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {status.replace(/_/g, " ")}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

File: src/app/api/account/addresses/route.ts

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { auth } from "@/auth";
import clientPromise from "@/lib/mongodb";
import { ObjectId } from "mongodb";

export async function GET() {
  const session = await auth();
  if (!session?.user) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const client = await clientPromise;
  const db = client.db();

  const userQuery = [
    ...(session.user.id ? [{ userId: session.user.id }] : []),
    ...(session.user.email
      ? [{ userEmail: session.user.email.toLowerCase().trim() }]
      : []),
  ];

  const addresses = await db
    .collection("addresses")
    .find({ $or: userQuery })
    .sort({ isDefault: -1, createdAt: -1 })
    .toArray();

  return NextResponse.json({ success: true, addresses });
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const session = await auth();
  if (!session?.user) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const body = await req.json();
  const client = await clientPromise;
  const db = client.db();

  // Handle both field naming schemes gracefully
  const fullName = body.fullName?.trim();
  const phone = (body.phone || body.phoneNumber)?.trim();
  const address = (body.address || body.street)?.trim();
  const city = body.city?.trim();
  const state = body.state?.trim();
  const pincode = (body.pincode || body.postalCode)?.trim();
  const type = body.type || "HOME";

  if (!fullName || !phone || !address || !city || !state || !pincode) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "All address fields are required" },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }

  const userQuery = [
    ...(session.user.id ? [{ userId: session.user.id }] : []),
    ...(session.user.email
      ? [{ userEmail: session.user.email.toLowerCase().trim() }]
      : []),
  ];

  // Count existing addresses to decide default state
  const existingCount = await db
    .collection("addresses")
    .countDocuments({ $or: userQuery });

  const shouldBeDefault = Boolean(body.isDefault) || existingCount === 0;

  // If this address is default, reset previous default addresses
  if (shouldBeDefault) {
    await db
      .collection("addresses")
      .updateMany({ $or: userQuery }, { $set: { isDefault: false } });
  }

  const newAddress = {
    userId: session.user.id || "",
    userEmail: session.user.email?.toLowerCase().trim() || "",
    type,
    fullName,
    phone,
    address,
    city,
    state,
    pincode,
    isDefault: shouldBeDefault,
    createdAt: new Date(),
    updatedAt: new Date(),
  };

  const result = await db.collection("addresses").insertOne(newAddress);

  return NextResponse.json(
    {
      success: true,
      address: { ...newAddress, _id: result.insertedId },
    },
    { status: 201 },
  );
}

export async function DELETE(req: Request) {
  const session = await auth();
  if (!session?.user) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const id = searchParams.get("id");
  if (!id) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Missing ID" }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const client = await clientPromise;
  const db = client.db();

  const userQuery = [
    ...(session.user.id ? [{ userId: session.user.id }] : []),
    ...(session.user.email
      ? [{ userEmail: session.user.email.toLowerCase().trim() }]
      : []),
  ];

  await db.collection("addresses").deleteOne({
    _id: new ObjectId(id),
    $or: userQuery,
  });

  return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
}

File: src/app/api/account/Orders/route.ts

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { auth } from "@/auth";
import clientPromise from "@/lib/mongodb";

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const session = await auth();
    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const localOrderIds: string[] = Array.isArray(body?.localOrderIds)
      ? body.localOrderIds
      : [];

    const client = await clientPromise;
    const db = client.db();

    const queryConditions: Record<string, any>[] = [];

    if (session?.user?.id) {
      queryConditions.push({ userId: session.user.id });
    }

    if (session?.user?.email) {
      const email = session.user.email.toLowerCase().trim();
      queryConditions.push({ userEmail: email });
      queryConditions.push({ "customer.email": email });
    }

    if (localOrderIds.length > 0) {
      queryConditions.push({ orderId: { $in: localOrderIds } });
    }

    if (queryConditions.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ orders: [] });
    }

    const orders = await db
      .collection("orders")
      .find({ $or: queryConditions })
      .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
      .toArray();

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, orders });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching account orders:", error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Internal Server Error" },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }
}

File: src/app/api/admin/delhivery/route.ts
================
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db";
import { Order } from "@/models/Order";

interface DelhiveryPackage {
  waybill: string;
}

interface DelhiveryResponse {
  packages?: DelhiveryPackage[];
  error?: string;
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { orderId, adminSecret } = await req.json();

    if (adminSecret !== process.env.ADMIN_SECRET) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const token = process.env.DELHIVERY_API_TOKEN;
    if (!token) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Delhivery API Token missing in .env.local" },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    await connectDB();
    const order = await Order.findOne({ orderId });
    if (!order) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Order not found" }, { status: 404 });
    }

    // Call Delhivery One API to create Surface parcel
    const payload = {
      shipments: [
        {
          name: order.customer.fullName,
          add: order.customer.address,
          pin: order.customer.pincode,
          city: order.customer.city,
          state: order.customer.state,
          country: "India",
          phone: order.customer.phone,
          order: order.orderId,
          payment_mode: order.paymentMethod === "COD" ? "COD" : "Pre-paid",
          products_desc: "Fragrance Bottle (Surface Transport)",
          total_amount: order.total,
          cod_amount: order.paymentMethod === "COD" ? order.total : 0,
          weight: 350, // estimated bottle + box in grams
        },
      ],
      pickup_location: {
        name: "CONSCENT WAREHOUSE", // Must match your Delhivery registered pickup address name
      },
    };

    const response = await fetch(
      "https://track.delhivery.com/api/cmu/create.json",
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          Authorization: `Token ${token}`,
        },
        body: `format=json&data=${JSON.stringify(payload)}`,
      },
    );

    const result: DelhiveryResponse = await response.json();

    if (result.packages && result.packages.length > 0) {
      const generatedAWB = result.packages[0].waybill;

      order.trackingNumber = generatedAWB;
      order.shippingStatus = "PACKED";
      await order.save();

      return NextResponse.json({ success: true, awb: generatedAWB });
    }

    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: result.error || "Failed to generate AWB from Delhivery" },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  } catch (error) {
    const message =
      error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Failed to generate AWB";
    return NextResponse.json({ error: message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

    return NextResponse.json({ error: message }, { status: 500 });

File: src/app/api/auth/register/route.ts

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import clientPromise from "@/lib/mongodb";
import bcrypt from "bcryptjs";

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { name, email, password } = await req.json();

    if (!name || !email || !password || password.length < 6) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Valid name, email and 6+ character password required." },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    const client = await clientPromise;
    const db = client.db();
    const normalizedEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();

    const existingUser = await db
      .collection("users")
      .findOne({ email: normalizedEmail });
    if (existingUser) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "An account with this email already exists." },
        { status: 409 },
      );
    }

    const hashedPassword = await bcrypt.hash(password, 12);

    const result = await db.collection("users").insertOne({
      name,
      email: normalizedEmail,
      password: hashedPassword,
      createdAt: new Date(),
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    });

    return NextResponse.json(
      { message: "Account created successfully", userId: result.insertedId },
      { status: 201 },
    );
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Internal server error" },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }
}

================
File: src/app/api/checkout/create-order/route.ts
================
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import Razorpay from "razorpay";
import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db";
import { Order } from "@/models/Order";
import { products } from "@/data/products";

interface ClientOrderItem {
  productId: string;
  name: string;
  size: string;
  qty: number;
}

const razorpay = new Razorpay({
  key_id: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_RAZORPAY_KEY_ID!,
  key_secret: process.env.RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET!,
});

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { customer, items }: { customer: unknown; items: ClientOrderItem[] } =
      await req.json();

    if (!customer || !items || items.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Missing required order details" },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    // Server-side price calculation to prevent client-side tampering
    let verifiedSubtotal = 0;
    const verifiedItems = items.map((clientItem) => {
      const dbProduct = products.find((p) => p.id === clientItem.productId);
      if (!dbProduct) throw new Error(`Product not found: ${clientItem.name}`);

      const dbVariant = dbProduct.variants.find(
        (v) => v.size === clientItem.size,
      );
      if (!dbVariant)
        throw new Error(`Variant not found for: ${clientItem.name}`);

      verifiedSubtotal += dbVariant.price * clientItem.qty;

      return {
        productId: dbProduct.id,
        slug: dbProduct.slug,
        name: dbProduct.name,
        size: dbVariant.size,
        price: dbVariant.price,
        qty: clientItem.qty,
        image:
          typeof dbProduct.image === "string"
            ? dbProduct.image
            : dbProduct.image.src,
      };
    });

    const shipping =
      verifiedSubtotal >= 1499 || verifiedSubtotal === 0 ? 0 : 99;
    const total = verifiedSubtotal + shipping;

    // 1. Create order on Razorpay (amount in paise: ₹1 = 100 paise)
    const razorpayOrder = await razorpay.orders.create({
      amount: total * 100,
      currency: "INR",
      receipt: `rcpt_${Date.now()}`,
    });

    // 2. Save order in MongoDB as PENDING
    await connectDB();
    const customOrderId = `CON-${Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000)}`;

    const newOrder = await Order.create({
      orderId: customOrderId,
      customer,
      items: verifiedItems,
      subtotal: verifiedSubtotal,
      shipping,
      total,
      paymentStatus: "PENDING",
      razorpayOrderId: razorpayOrder.id,
    });

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      razorpayOrderId: razorpayOrder.id,
      amount: razorpayOrder.amount,
      currency: razorpayOrder.currency,
      dbOrderId: newOrder.orderId,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const message =
      error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Failed to create order";
    console.error("Order creation error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

File: src/app/api/checkout/verify/route.ts
================
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import crypto from "crypto";
import { Resend } from "resend";
import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db";
import { Order } from "@/models/Order";
import { formatINR } from "@/data/products";

interface OrderItem {
  name: string;
  size: string;
  qty: number;
  price: number;
}

const resend = new Resend(process.env.RESEND_API_KEY);

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { razorpayOrderId, razorpayPaymentId, razorpaySignature, dbOrderId } =
      await req.json();

    // Verify HMAC SHA256 Signature
    const body = `${razorpayOrderId}|${razorpayPaymentId}`;
    const expectedSignature = crypto
      .createHmac("sha256", process.env.RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET!)
      .update(body.toString())
      .digest("hex");

    if (expectedSignature !== razorpaySignature) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Invalid payment signature" },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    await connectDB();

    // Generate starter surface AWB code
    const autoAWB = `DLV${Date.now().toString().slice(-8)}`;

    const updatedOrder = await Order.findOneAndUpdate(
      { orderId: dbOrderId },
      {
        paymentStatus: "PAID",
        razorpayPaymentId,
        shippingStatus: "ORDER_PLACED",
        trackingNumber: autoAWB,
      },
      { new: true },
    );

    if (!updatedOrder) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Order record not found" },
        { status: 404 },
      );
    }

    // Send Confirmation Email via Resend
    if (process.env.RESEND_API_KEY) {
      try {
        const itemsListHtml = updatedOrder.items
          .map(
            (i: OrderItem) =>
              `<tr>
                <td style="padding: 8px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid #eee;"><strong>${i.name}</strong> (${i.size}) x ${i.qty}</td>
                <td style="padding: 8px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid #eee; text-align: right;">${formatINR(i.price * i.qty)}</td>
              </tr>`,
          )
          .join("");

        await resend.emails.send({
          from: "CONSCENT <onboarding@resend.dev>", // replace with your verified domain in production
          to: updatedOrder.customer.email,
          subject: `Order Confirmed: ${updatedOrder.orderId}`,
          html: `
            <div style="font-family: sans-serif; max-width: 600px; margin: auto; padding: 24px; border: 1px solid #e5e7eb; border-radius: 8px; color: #111;">
              <h1 style="letter-spacing: 0.15em; font-size: 24px; text-transform: uppercase; margin-bottom: 4px;">CONSCENT</h1>
              <p style="color: #666; font-size: 14px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.1em; margin-top: 0;">Order Confirmation</p>
              
              <div style="background-color: #f9fafb; padding: 16px; border-radius: 6px; margin: 20px 0;">
                <p style="margin: 0; font-size: 14px;"><strong>Order ID:</strong> ${updatedOrder.orderId}</p>
                <p style="margin: 4px 0 0; font-size: 14px;"><strong>Payment ID:</strong> ${updatedOrder.razorpayPaymentId}</p>
                <p style="margin: 4px 0 0; font-size: 14px;"><strong>Status:</strong> Order Placed (Surface Transit)</p>
              </div>

              <h3 style="font-size: 16px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.1em; margin-bottom: 8px;">Shipment Summary</h3>
              <table style="width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size: 14px;">
                <tbody>
                  ${itemsListHtml}
                </tbody>
              </table>

              <div style="margin-top: 16px; text-align: right; font-size: 14px;">
                <p style="margin: 4px 0;">Subtotal: <strong>${formatINR(updatedOrder.subtotal)}</strong></p>
                <p style="margin: 4px 0;">Shipping: <strong>${updatedOrder.shipping === 0 ? "FREE" : formatINR(updatedOrder.shipping)}</strong></p>
                <p style="margin: 8px 0; font-size: 18px; color: #e11d48;">Total Paid: <strong>${formatINR(updatedOrder.total)}</strong></p>
              </div>

              <div style="border-top: 1px solid #e5e7eb; margin-top: 24px; padding-top: 16px; font-size: 13px; color: #555;">
                <p style="margin: 0;"><strong>Shipping To:</strong></p>
                <p style="margin: 4px 0;">${updatedOrder.customer.fullName}</p>
                <p style="margin: 0;">${updatedOrder.customer.address}, ${updatedOrder.customer.city}, ${updatedOrder.customer.state} - ${updatedOrder.customer.pincode}</p>
                <p style="margin: 4px 0;">Phone: ${updatedOrder.customer.phone}</p>
              </div>

              <div style="text-align: center; margin-top: 32px;">
                <a href="${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || "http://localhost:3000"}/track?orderId=${updatedOrder.orderId}" 
                   style="background-color: #000; color: #fff; text-decoration: none; padding: 12px 24px; font-size: 12px; font-weight: bold; letter-spacing: 0.15em; text-transform: uppercase; border-radius: 4px; display: inline-block;">
                  Track Your Package
                </a>
              </div>
            </div>
          `,
        });
      } catch (emailErr) {
        console.error("Resend error:", emailErr);
      }
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      orderId: updatedOrder.orderId,
      trackingNumber: updatedOrder.trackingNumber,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const message =
      error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Payment verification failed";
    console.error("Payment verification error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

================
File: src/app/api/track/route.ts
================
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db";
import { Order } from "@/models/Order";

interface ShippingUpdateFields {
  shippingStatus: string;
  trackingNumber?: string;
}

// GET: Secure order lookup
export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    const orderId = searchParams.get("orderId");
    const phone = searchParams.get("phone");
    const ids = searchParams.get("ids"); // comma-separated IDs from local history

    await connectDB();

    // 1. Fetch user's personal order history from their stored IDs
    if (ids) {
      const orderIdList = ids.split(",").map((id) => id.trim().toUpperCase());
      const orders = await Order.find({ orderId: { $in: orderIdList } })
        .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
        .lean();
      return NextResponse.json({ success: true, orders });
    }

    // 2. Strict lookup requires both Order ID AND Customer Phone to prevent snooping
    if (!orderId || !phone) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Order ID and registered Phone number are both required." },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    const order = await Order.findOne({
      orderId: orderId.trim().toUpperCase(),
      "customer.phone": phone.trim(),
    }).lean();

    if (!order) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "No matching order found with this phone number." },
        { status: 404 },
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, order });
  } catch (error) {
    const message =
      error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Failed to fetch order status";
    return NextResponse.json({ error: message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

// PATCH: Admin status updates
export async function PATCH(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { orderId, shippingStatus, trackingNumber, adminSecret } =
      await req.json();

    if (adminSecret !== process.env.ADMIN_SECRET) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Unauthorized access" },
        { status: 401 },
      );
    }

    await connectDB();

    const updateFields: ShippingUpdateFields = { shippingStatus };
    if (trackingNumber) {
      updateFields.trackingNumber = trackingNumber;
    }

    const updated = await Order.findOneAndUpdate(
      { orderId },
      { $set: updateFields },
      { new: true },
    );

    if (!updated) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Order not found" }, { status: 404 });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, order: updated });
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Update failed";
    return NextResponse.json({ error: message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
