"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useSession } from "next-auth/react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Check, Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { formatINR } from "@/data/products";
import { cartSubtotal, useCart } from "@/lib/store/cart";
import { useHydrated } from "@/hooks/use-hydrated";

export const INDIAN_STATES = [
  "Andaman and Nicobar Islands",
  "Andhra Pradesh",
  "Arunachal Pradesh",
  "Assam",
  "Bihar",
  "Chandigarh",
  "Chhattisgarh",
  "Dadra and Nagar Haveli and Daman and Diu",
  "Delhi",
  "Goa",
  "Gujarat",
  "Haryana",
  "Himachal Pradesh",
  "Jammu and Kashmir",
  "Jharkhand",
  "Karnataka",
  "Kerala",
  "Ladakh",
  "Lakshadweep",
  "Madhya Pradesh",
  "Maharashtra",
  "Manipur",
  "Meghalaya",
  "Mizoram",
  "Nagaland",
  "Odisha",
  "Puducherry",
  "Punjab",
  "Rajasthan",
  "Sikkim",
  "Tamil Nadu",
  "Telangana",
  "Tripura",
  "Uttar Pradesh",
  "Uttarakhand",
  "West Bengal",
] as const;

interface RazorpayResponse {
  razorpay_order_id: string;
  razorpay_payment_id: string;
  razorpay_signature: string;
}

interface RazorpayOptions {
  key?: string;
  amount: number;
  currency: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  order_id: string;
  callback_url?: string;
  redirect?: boolean;
  prefill: {
    name: string;
    email: string;
    contact: string;
  };
  theme: {
    color: string;
  };
  handler: (response: RazorpayResponse) => void | Promise<void>;
  modal?: {
    ondismiss?: () => void;
  };
}

interface RazorpayInstance {
  open: () => void;
}

interface RazorpayConstructor {
  new (options: RazorpayOptions): RazorpayInstance;
}

declare global {
  interface Window {
    Razorpay?: RazorpayConstructor;
  }
}

interface SavedAddress {
  _id: string;
  type: "HOME" | "WORK" | "OTHER";
  fullName: string;
  phone: string;
  address: string;
  city: string;
  state: string;
  pincode: string;
  isDefault: boolean;
}

function isValidIndianPincode(pin: string): boolean {
  const clean = pin.replace(/\D/g, "");
  return clean.length === 6 && /^[1-9][0-9]{5}\$/.test(clean);
}

function isValidIndianPhone(phone: string): boolean {
  const digits = phone.replace(/\D/g, "");
  return digits.length >= 10 && /^[6-9]\d{9}\$/.test(digits.slice(-10));
}

export default function CheckoutPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { data: session, status } = useSession();
  const items = useCart((s) => s.items);
  const clearCart = useCart((s) => s.clear);
  const hydrated = useHydrated();

  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [addresses, setAddresses] = useState<SavedAddress[]>([]);
  const [selectedAddressId, setSelectedAddressId] = useState<string | null>(
    null,
  );
  const [showNewAddressForm, setShowNewAddressForm] = useState(false);
  const [saveToAccount, setSaveToAccount] = useState(true);
  const [addressType, setAddressType] = useState<"HOME" | "WORK" | "OTHER">(
    "HOME",
  );

  const subtotal = cartSubtotal(items);
  const shipping = subtotal === 0 || subtotal >= 1499 ? 0 : 99;
  const total = subtotal + shipping;

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    fullName: "",
    email: "",
    phone: "",
    address: "",
    city: "",
    state: "Delhi",
    pincode: "",
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === "unauthenticated") {
      router.push("/account/login?callbackUrl=/checkout");
    }
  }, [status, router]);

  const applyAddress = useCallback(
    (addr: SavedAddress) => {
      setSelectedAddressId(addr._id);
      setFormData({
        fullName: addr.fullName,
        email: session?.user?.email || "",
        phone: addr.phone.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(-10),
        address: addr.address,
        city: addr.city,
        state: addr.state || "Delhi",
        pincode: addr.pincode.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 6),
      });
    },
    [session?.user?.email],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status !== "authenticated") return;

    let isMounted = true;
    async function loadAddresses() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/account/addresses");
        const data = await res.json();

        if (!isMounted) return;

        if (res.ok && data.addresses && data.addresses.length > 0) {
          setAddresses(data.addresses);
          const defaultAddress =
            data.addresses.find((a: SavedAddress) => a.isDefault) ||
            data.addresses[0];
          applyAddress(defaultAddress);
          setShowNewAddressForm(false);
        } else {
          setFormData((prev) => ({
            ...prev,
            fullName: prev.fullName || session?.user?.name || "",
            email: prev.email || session?.user?.email || "",
          }));
          setShowNewAddressForm(true);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Failed to load addresses:", err);
        if (isMounted) setShowNewAddressForm(true);
      }
    }

    loadAddresses();
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [status, session?.user?.name, session?.user?.email, applyAddress]);

  const handleSelectSaved = (addr: SavedAddress) => {
    applyAddress(addr);
    setShowNewAddressForm(false);
  };

  const onSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();

    const cleanPin = formData.pincode.replace(/\D/g, "");
    const cleanPhone = formData.phone.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(-10);

    if (!isValidIndianPincode(cleanPin)) {
      toast.error("Please enter a valid 6-digit Indian PIN code (e.g. 110025)");
      return;
    }

    if (!isValidIndianPhone(cleanPhone)) {
      toast.error("Please enter a valid 10-digit Indian mobile number");
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);

    const submissionData = {
      ...formData,
      phone: cleanPhone,
      pincode: cleanPin,
    };

    if (showNewAddressForm && saveToAccount) {
      try {
        await fetch("/api/account/addresses", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            fullName: submissionData.fullName,
            phone: submissionData.phone,
            address: submissionData.address,
            city: submissionData.city,
            state: submissionData.state,
            pincode: submissionData.pincode,
            type: addressType,
            isDefault: addresses.length === 0,
          }),
        });
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("Address auto-save error:", e);
      }
    }

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/checkout/create-order", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          customer: submissionData,
          items: items.map((i) => ({
            productId: i.productId,
            name: i.name,
            size: i.size,
            qty: i.qty,
          })),
        }),
      });

      const orderData = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(orderData.error || "Order initiation failed");
      }

      const options: RazorpayOptions = {
        key: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_RAZORPAY_KEY_ID,
        amount: orderData.amount,
        currency: orderData.currency,
        name: "CONSCENT",
        description: "Artisan Fragrance Purchase",
        order_id: orderData.razorpayOrderId,
        prefill: {
          name: submissionData.fullName,
          email: submissionData.email,
          contact: submissionData.phone,
        },
        theme: {
          color: "#E11D48",
        },
        handler: async function (response: RazorpayResponse) {
          try {
            const verifyRes = await fetch("/api/checkout/verify", {
              method: "POST",
              headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
              body: JSON.stringify({
                razorpayOrderId: response.razorpay_order_id,
                razorpayPaymentId: response.razorpay_payment_id,
                razorpaySignature: response.razorpay_signature,
                dbOrderId: orderData.dbOrderId,
              }),
            });

            const verifyData = await verifyRes.json();
            clearCart();

            const finalOrderId = verifyData?.orderId || orderData.dbOrderId;
            try {
              const savedOrders: string[] = JSON.parse(
                localStorage.getItem("my_orders") || "[]",
              );
              if (!savedOrders.includes(finalOrderId)) {
                savedOrders.unshift(finalOrderId);
                localStorage.setItem("my_orders", JSON.stringify(savedOrders));
              }
            } catch (e) {
              console.error("Local storage sync error:", e);
            }

            router.push(
              `/thank-you?orderId=${encodeURIComponent(finalOrderId)}`,
            );
          } catch (verifyError) {
            console.error("Verification error:", verifyError);
            router.push(
              `/thank-you?orderId=${encodeURIComponent(orderData.dbOrderId)}`,
            );
          } finally {
            setSubmitting(false);
          }
        },
        modal: {
          ondismiss: function () {
            setSubmitting(false);
          },
        },
      };

      if (!window.Razorpay) {
        toast.error("Razorpay SDK failed to load. Please refresh.");
        setSubmitting(false);
        return;
      }
      const paymentObject = new window.Razorpay(options);
      paymentObject.open();
    } catch (err) {
      const message =
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to process payment";
      toast.error(message);
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (status === "loading" || (!hydrated && items.length === 0)) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[60vh] w-full max-w-[1400px] items-center justify-center px-4">
        <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.3em] text-muted-foreground animate-pulse">
          Loading checkout...
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1400px] px-4 py-12 sm:px-6">
      <h1 className="text-7xl sm:text-8xl">
        Check<span className="text-brand">out</span>
      </h1>

      {hydrated && items.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="border-border mt-10 border p-12 text-center">
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm tracking-[0.2em] uppercase">
            Your bag is empty
          </p>
          <Link
            href="/parfums"
            className="bg-ink text-background hover:bg-brand mt-6 inline-block cursor-pointer px-8 py-4 text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase transition-colors"
          >
            Shop parfums
          </Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="mt-10 grid gap-10 lg:grid-cols-[1.2fr_0.8fr]">
          <div className="border-border border p-6 sm:p-8">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border pb-4">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-display uppercase tracking-wide">
                  Shipping Details
                </h2>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground tracking-wider uppercase mt-0.5">
                  Logged in as {session?.user?.email}
                </p>
              </div>

              {addresses.length > 0 && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowNewAddressForm((prev) => !prev)}
                  className="cursor-pointer text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.2em] uppercase text-brand hover:opacity-80 flex items-center gap-1 transition"
                >
                  {showNewAddressForm ? (
                    "Use Saved"
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Plus className="size-3.5" /> New Address
                    </>
                  )}
                </button>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Saved Address Cards */}
            {!showNewAddressForm && addresses.length > 0 && (
              <div className="mt-6 space-y-3">
                <span className="text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase text-muted-foreground block mb-2">
                  Select Delivery Address
                </span>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  {addresses.map((addr) => {
                    const isSelected = selectedAddressId === addr._id;
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={addr._id}
                        onClick={() => handleSelectSaved(addr)}
                        className={`cursor-pointer relative p-4 border transition-all ${
                          isSelected
                            ? "border-brand bg-brand/5 ring-1 ring-brand"
                            : "border-border hover:border-foreground/40 bg-card"
                        }`}
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold tracking-[0.2em] uppercase bg-ink text-background px-2 py-0.5">
                            {addr.type}
                          </span>
                          {isSelected && (
                            <span className="text-brand flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-bold tracking-wider uppercase">
                              <Check className="size-3.5" /> Deliver Here
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
                          {addr.fullName}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1 line-clamp-2">
                          {addr.address}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          {addr.city}, {addr.state} — {addr.pincode}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground mt-2">
                          +91 {addr.phone}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Address Form */}
            <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="mt-6">
              {showNewAddressForm && (
                <>
                  <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-4">
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase text-muted-foreground">
                      Tag As:
                    </span>
                    {(["HOME", "WORK", "OTHER"] as const).map((t) => (
                      <button
                        key={t}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setAddressType(t)}
                        className={`cursor-pointer px-3 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold tracking-widest uppercase border transition-colors ${
                          addressType === t
                            ? "bg-brand text-brand-foreground border-brand"
                            : "border-border text-muted-foreground hover:border-foreground"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {t}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase">
                        Full Name
                      </span>
                      <input
                        required
                        type="text"
                        value={formData.fullName}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          setFormData({ ...formData, fullName: e.target.value })
                        }
                        autoComplete="name"
                        className="border-input focus:border-brand mt-2 w-full border bg-transparent px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase">
                        Email Address
                      </span>
                      <input
                        required
                        type="email"
                        value={formData.email}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          setFormData({ ...formData, email: e.target.value })
                        }
                        autoComplete="email"
                        className="border-input focus:border-brand mt-2 w-full border bg-transparent px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase">
                        Mobile Number
                      </span>
                      <input
                        required
                        type="tel"
                        maxLength={10}
                        value={formData.phone}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          setFormData({
                            ...formData,
                            phone: e.target.value
                              .replace(/\D/g, "")
                              .slice(0, 10),
                          })
                        }
                        autoComplete="tel"
                        placeholder="7982846176"
                        className="border-input focus:border-brand mt-2 w-full border bg-transparent px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase">
                        City
                      </span>
                      <input
                        required
                        type="text"
                        value={formData.city}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          setFormData({ ...formData, city: e.target.value })
                        }
                        autoComplete="address-level2"
                        className="border-input focus:border-brand mt-2 w-full border bg-transparent px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase">
                        Street Address / Flat / Floor
                      </span>
                      <input
                        required
                        type="text"
                        value={formData.address}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          setFormData({ ...formData, address: e.target.value })
                        }
                        autoComplete="street-address"
                        className="border-input focus:border-brand mt-2 w-full border bg-transparent px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase">
                        State
                      </span>
                      <select
                        required
                        value={formData.state}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          setFormData({ ...formData, state: e.target.value })
                        }
                        className="border-input focus:border-brand mt-2 w-full border bg-background px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        {INDIAN_STATES.map((st) => (
                          <option key={st} value={st}>
                            {st}
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase">
                        PIN Code (6 digits)
                      </span>
                      <input
                        required
                        type="text"
                        maxLength={6}
                        value={formData.pincode}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          setFormData({
                            ...formData,
                            pincode: e.target.value
                              .replace(/\D/g, "")
                              .slice(0, 6),
                          })
                        }
                        autoComplete="postal-code"
                        placeholder="110025"
                        className="border-input focus:border-brand mt-2 w-full border bg-transparent px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <label className="mt-5 flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={saveToAccount}
                      onChange={(e) => setSaveToAccount(e.target.checked)}
                      className="size-4 accent-brand cursor-pointer"
                    />
                    <span className="text-xs tracking-wider text-muted-foreground uppercase">
                      Save this address to your account for 1-click checkout
                    </span>
                  </label>
                </>
              )}

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={submitting}
                className="bg-brand text-brand-foreground mt-8 w-full cursor-pointer py-5 text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.3em] uppercase transition-transform hover:-translate-y-0.5 disabled:opacity-60"
              >
                {submitting ? "Processing…" : `Pay ${formatINR(total)}`}
              </button>
              <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-3 text-center text-[11px] tracking-[0.15em] uppercase">
                Secure UPI · Cards · NetBanking by Razorpay
              </p>
            </form>
          </div>

          <aside className="border-border h-fit border p-6 sm:p-8">
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-display uppercase tracking-wide">
              Order summary
            </h2>
            <div className="mt-6 space-y-4">
              {hydrated &&
                items.map((i) => {
                  const itemImg =
                    typeof i.image === "string" ? i.image : i.image.src;
                  return (
                    <div key={i.key} className="flex items-center gap-4">
                      <img
                        src={itemImg}
                        alt={i.name}
                        width={1008}
                        height={1200}
                        loading="lazy"
                        className="bg-tile size-16 object-cover"
                      />
                      <div className="flex-1">
                        <p className="text-sm font-bold tracking-wide uppercase">
                          {i.name}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-[11px] tracking-[0.2em] uppercase">
                          {i.size} × {i.qty}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-sm font-bold">
                        {formatINR(i.price * i.qty)}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
            </div>

            <div className="border-border mt-6 space-y-2 border-t pt-6 text-sm">
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span className="tracking-[0.15em] uppercase">Subtotal</span>
                <span className="font-bold">{formatINR(subtotal)}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span className="tracking-[0.15em] uppercase">Shipping</span>
                <span className="font-bold">
                  {shipping === 0 ? "FREE" : formatINR(shipping)}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="border-border mt-3 flex justify-between border-t pt-3">
                <span className="text-lg font-bold tracking-[0.15em] uppercase">
                  Total
                </span>
                <span className="text-2xl font-bold">{formatINR(total)}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </aside>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Script from "next/script";
import { Bebas_Neue, Inter } from "next/font/google";
import "./globals.css";
import { GoogleAnalytics } from "@next/third-parties/google";

import { Providers } from "@/components/Providers";
import { Navbar } from "@/components/Navbar";
import { Footer } from "@/components/Footer";
import { CartDrawer } from "@/components/CartDrawer";
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/sonner";

const bebasNeue = Bebas_Neue({
  weight: "400",
  subsets: ["latin"],
  variable: "--font-display",
});

const inter = Inter({
  subsets: ["latin"],
  variable: "--font-sans",
});

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  metadataBase: new URL(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || "https://conscent.in",
  ),
  title: {
    default: "CONSCENT — Loud Fragrance",
    template: "%s | CONSCENT",
  },

    "Bold artisan extrait de parfum and high-concentration fragrances crafted in small batches.",

    title: "CONSCENT — Loud Fragrance",

    siteName: "CONSCENT",

  },

  children,

}) {

    <html lang="en" className={`${bebasNeue.variable} ${inter.variable}`}>
      <body className="flex min-h-screen flex-col bg-background font-sans text-foreground antialiased">

================
File: src/app/parfums/[slug]/page.tsx
================
"use client";

import { use, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { formatINR, products } from "@/data/products";
import { useCart } from "@/lib/store/cart";

export default function ParfumDetailPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ slug: string }>;
}) {
  const { slug } = use(params);
  const product = products.find((p) => p.slug === slug);

  if (!product) {
    notFound();
  }

  const [selectedVariant, setSelectedVariant] = useState(product.variants[0]);
  const addItem = useCart((s) => s.addItem);

  const imgSrc =
    typeof product.image === "string" ? product.image : product.image.src;

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1400px] px-4 py-12 sm:px-6">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold tracking-[0.2em] uppercase text-muted-foreground">
        <Link href="/parfums" className="hover:text-foreground">
          Parfums
        </Link>
        <span>/</span>
        <span className="text-foreground">{product.name}</span>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-12 lg:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="border border-border bg-tile">
          <img
            src={imgSrc}
            alt={product.name}
            width={1008}
            height={1200}
            className="aspect-[4/5] w-full object-cover"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col justify-center">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase text-brand">
            {product.family} · {product.intensity} INTENSITY
          </span>

                Surface Road

            <span className="text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.2em] uppercase">
              Select Size

                    selectedVariant.id === v.id
                      ? "border-brand bg-brand text-brand-foreground"
                      : "border-border bg-transparent hover:border-foreground"
                  }`}
                >
                  {v.size} — {formatINR(v.price)}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-8 flex items-baseline gap-3">
            <span className="text-4xl font-bold">
              {formatINR(selectedVariant.price)}
            </span>
            {selectedVariant.compareAt && (
              <span className="text-base text-muted-foreground line-through">
                {formatINR(selectedVariant.compareAt)}
              </span>
            )}

            onClick={() => {
              addItem(product, selectedVariant);

                `${product.name} (${selectedVariant.size}) added to bag`,
              );

            Add to bag
          </button>

          <div className="border-border mt-12 border-t pt-8">
            <h3 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-wide uppercase">
              Olfactory Notes
            </h3>
            <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-3 gap-4">

                  Top
                </h4>
                <ul className="mt-2 text-sm space-y-1">
                  {product.notes
                    .filter((n) => n.layer === "top")
                    .map((n) => (
                      <li key={n.name}>{n.name}</li>
                    ))}
                </ul>
              </div>

File: src/app/parfums/page.tsx

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { ProductCard } from "@/components/ProductCard";
import { products } from "@/data/products";

const families = ["ALL", ...Array.from(new Set(products.map((p) => p.family)))];
const genders = ["ALL", "UNISEX", "MASC", "FEM"] as const;
const intensities = ["ALL", "LIGHT", "MEDIUM", "HEAVY"] as const;

function Chip({
  active,
  onClick,
  children,
}: {
  active: boolean;
  onClick: () => void;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      onClick={onClick}
      className={`border px-4 py-2 text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[0.2em] uppercase transition-colors ${
        active
          ? "bg-ink text-background border-ink"
          : "border-border hover:border-ink text-foreground"
      }`}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

export default function CatalogPage() {
  const [family, setFamily] = useState("ALL");
  const [gender, setGender] = useState<string>("ALL");
  const [intensity, setIntensity] = useState<string>("ALL");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<"featured" | "low" | "high">("featured");

  const list = useMemo(() => {
    const filtered = products.filter(
      (p) =>
        (family === "ALL" || p.family === family) &&
        (gender === "ALL" || p.gender === gender) &&
        (intensity === "ALL" || p.intensity === intensity),
    );
    const price = (p: (typeof products)[number]) =>
      Math.min(...p.variants.map((v) => v.price));
    if (sort === "low")
      return [...filtered].sort((a, b) => price(a) - price(b));
    if (sort === "high")
      return [...filtered].sort((a, b) => price(b) - price(a));
    return filtered;
  }, [family, gender, intensity, sort]);

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="bg-ink text-background">
        <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1400px] px-4 py-14 sm:px-6">
          <h1 className="text-7xl sm:text-8xl">
            Par<span className="text-brand">fums</span>
          </h1>
          <p className="text-background/60 mt-3 text-sm tracking-[0.2em] uppercase">
            {products.length} scents in rotation
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1400px] px-4 py-10 sm:px-6">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {families.map((f) => (
              <Chip key={f} active={family === f} onClick={() => setFamily(f)}>
                {f}
              </Chip>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {genders.map((g) => (
              <Chip key={g} active={gender === g} onClick={() => setGender(g)}>
                {g === "ALL" ? "Anyone" : g}
              </Chip>
            ))}
            {intensities.map((i) => (
              <Chip
                key={i}
                active={intensity === i}
                onClick={() => setIntensity(i)}
              >
                {i === "ALL" ? "Any strength" : i}
              </Chip>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="border-border mt-8 flex items-center justify-between border-y py-3">
          <span className="text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase">
            {list.length} result{list.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}
          </span>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="text-muted-foreground text-[11px] tracking-[0.2em] uppercase">
              Sort
            </span>
            <select
              value={sort}
              onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value as typeof sort)}
              className="border-border bg-background border px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[0.15em] uppercase"
            >
              <option value="featured">Featured</option>
              <option value="low">Price: low to high</option>
              <option value="high">Price: high to low</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>

        {list.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-muted-foreground py-24 text-center text-sm tracking-[0.2em] uppercase">
            Nothing matches those filters
          </p>
        ) : (
          <div className="mt-8 grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
            {list.map((p) => (
              <ProductCard key={p.id} product={p} />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { MetadataRoute } from "next";

export default function robots(): MetadataRoute.Robots {
  const baseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || "https://conscent-psi.vercel.app";
  return {
    rules: {
      userAgent: "*", //all crawling bots
      allow: "/", //pages that are allowed to bots
      disallow: ["/admin", "/api/"], //pages not to be crawled by bots
    },
    sitemap: `${baseUrl}/sitemap.xml`,
  };
}

File: src/app/sitemap.ts

import { MetadataRoute } from "next";
import { products } from "@/data/products";

export default function sitemap(): MetadataRoute.Sitemap {
  const baseUrl =
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || "https://conscent-psi.vercel.app";

  const productUrls = products.map((p) => ({
    url: `${baseUrl}/parfums/${p.slug}`,
    lastModified: new Date(),
    changeFrequency: "weekly" as const,
    priority: 0.8,
  }));

  const staticUrls = [
    "",
    "/parfums",
    "/about",
    "/terms",
    "/privacy",
    "/contact",
  ].map((route) => ({
    url: `${baseUrl}${route}`,
    lastModified: new Date(),
    changeFrequency: "monthly" as const,
    priority: route === "" ? 1.0 : 0.6,
  }));

  return [...staticUrls, ...productUrls];
}

"use client";

import { useSearchParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Suspense, useEffect, useState } from "react";

function ThankYouContent() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const router = useRouter();
  const orderId = searchParams.get("orderId");
  const [secondsLeft, setSecondsLeft] = useState(5);

  // 1. Countdown timer
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!orderId) return;

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setSecondsLeft((prev) => (prev > 0 ? prev - 1 : 0));
    }, 1000);

    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [orderId]);

  // 2. Safe redirect when countdown hits 0
  useEffect(() => {
    if (orderId && secondsLeft === 0) {
      router.replace(`/track?orderId=${encodeURIComponent(orderId)}`);
    }
  }, [secondsLeft, orderId, router]);

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto flex min-h-[65vh] max-w-xl flex-col items-center justify-center px-4 py-16 text-center sm:px-6">
      <h1 className="display text-7xl sm:text-8xl">
        Thank <span className="text-brand">You</span>
      </h1>

      <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-4 max-w-md text-sm sm:text-base">
        Your order is placed. We’re packing your perfumes and preparing them for
        dispatch.
      </p>

      {orderId && (
        <div className="border-border bg-tile/50 mt-8 w-full border-y p-6">
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em]">
            Order ID
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-2xl font-bold tracking-wider text-foreground">
            {orderId}
          </p>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-2 text-xs">
            We sent a receipt with the details to your email.
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-6 text-xs uppercase tracking-widest">
        Taking you to your orders in{" "}
        <span className="font-mono text-sm font-bold text-foreground">
          {secondsLeft}s
        </span>
      </p>

      <div className="mt-8 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-4">
        <button
          onClick={() =>
            router.replace(
              `/track?orderId=${encodeURIComponent(orderId || "")}`,
            )
          }
          className="bg-brand text-brand-foreground cursor-pointer px-8 py-4 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.25em] transition-transform hover:-translate-y-0.5"
        >
          View Order Now
        </button>

        <Link
          href="/parfums"
          className="border-border hover:border-foreground border px-8 py-4 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.25em] transition-colors"
        >
          Continue Shopping
        </Link>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

export default function ThankYouPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense
      fallback={
        <div className="py-24 text-center text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.25em]">
          Loading...
        </div>
      }
    >
      <ThankYouContent />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

================
File: src/app/track/page.tsx
================
"use client";

import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useState, Suspense } from "react";
import { formatINR } from "@/data/products";
import {
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock,
  Package,
  Truck,
  ExternalLink,
} from "lucide-react";

function TrackContent() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const selectedOrderId = searchParams.get("orderId");
  interface TrackItem {
    name: string;
    size: string;
    qty: number;
    price: number;
  }

  interface TrackOrder {
    orderId: string;
    paymentStatus: string;
    shippingStatus: "ORDER_PLACED" | "PACKED" | "IN_TRANSIT" | "DELIVERED";
    trackingNumber?: string;
    total: number;
    createdAt: string;
    items: TrackItem[];
  }

  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<TrackOrder[]>([]);
  const [activeOrder, setActiveOrder] = useState<TrackOrder | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let ignore = false;
    const myOrderIds: string[] = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem("my_orders") || "[]",
    );

    if (myOrderIds.length === 0) {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        if (!ignore) setLoading(false);
      }, 0);
      return () => {
        ignore = true;
        clearTimeout(timer);
      };
    }

    fetch(`/api/track?ids=${encodeURIComponent(myOrderIds.join(","))}`)
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data: { success?: boolean; orders?: TrackOrder[] }) => {
        if (!ignore && data.success && data.orders) {
          setOrders(data.orders);
          const current =
            data.orders.find((o) => o.orderId === selectedOrderId) ||
            data.orders[0];
          setActiveOrder(current);
        }
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!ignore) setLoading(false);
      });

    return () => {
      ignore = true;
    };
  }, [selectedOrderId]);
  const steps = [
    { label: "Order Placed", status: "ORDER_PLACED", icon: Clock },
    { label: "Packed", status: "PACKED", icon: Package },
    { label: "In Transit", status: "IN_TRANSIT", icon: Truck },
    { label: "Delivered", status: "DELIVERED", icon: CheckCircle2 },
  ];

  const currentStepIndex =
    steps.findIndex((s) => s.status === activeOrder?.shippingStatus) || 0;

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1200px] px-4 py-16 sm:px-6">
      <h1 className="text-7xl sm:text-8xl">
        Your <span className="text-brand">Orders</span>
      </h1>
      <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-3 text-sm tracking-[0.2em] uppercase">
        Personal order tracking history
      </p>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="py-20 text-center uppercase tracking-widest text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          Loading your orders…
        </div>
      ) : orders.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="border border-border mt-10 p-12 text-center">
          <p className="text-sm uppercase tracking-widest text-muted-foreground">
            No recent orders found on this device.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="mt-10 grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[340px_1fr]">
          {/* Order History Tabs */}
          <aside className="border border-border divide-y divide-border h-fit">
            <div className="p-4 bg-tile">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold tracking-widest uppercase">
                Past Purchases
              </span>
            </div>
            {orders.map((o) => (
              <button
                key={o.orderId}
                onClick={() => setActiveOrder(o)}
                className={`w-full text-left p-4 transition-colors ${
                  activeOrder?.orderId === o.orderId
                    ? "bg-brand/10 border-l-4 border-l-brand"
                    : "hover:bg-tile"
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex justify-between items-baseline">
                  <span className="font-bold text-sm">{o.orderId}</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-brand">
                    {o.shippingStatus.replace(/_/g, " ")}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs mt-1">
                  {new Date(o.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("en-IN", {
                    month: "short",
                    day: "numeric",
                  })}{" "}
                  · {formatINR(o.total)}
                </p>
              </button>
            ))}
          </aside>

          {/* Active Order Details */}
          {activeOrder && (
            <div className="border border-border p-6 sm:p-10">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between border-b border-border pb-6 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold tracking-widest text-muted-foreground uppercase">
                    Order ID
                  </span>
                  <p className="text-2xl font-bold">{activeOrder.orderId}</p>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold tracking-widest text-muted-foreground uppercase">
                    Payment
                  </span>
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold text-brand uppercase">
                    {activeOrder.paymentStatus}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold tracking-widest text-muted-foreground uppercase">
                    Delhivery Surface AWB
                  </span>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <p className="text-sm font-mono font-bold">
                      {activeOrder.trackingNumber || "Assigning Carrier"}
                    </p>
                    {activeOrder.trackingNumber && (
                      <a
                        href={`https://www.delhivery.com/track/package/${activeOrder.trackingNumber}`}
                        target="_blank"
                        rel="noreferrer"
                        className="text-brand hover:underline flex items-center text-xs"
                      >
                        <ExternalLink className="size-3.5" />
                      </a>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Progress Steps */}
              <div className="my-10 grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-4">
                {steps.map((step, idx) => {
                  const Icon = step.icon;
                  const isDone = idx <= currentStepIndex;
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={step.label}
                      className={`border p-4 text-center transition-colors ${
                        isDone
                          ? "border-brand bg-brand/5 text-foreground"
                          : "border-border opacity-40 text-muted-foreground"
                      }`}
                    >
                      <Icon
                        className={`mx-auto size-6 ${isDone ? "text-brand" : ""}`}
                      />
                      <p className="mt-2 text-xs font-bold tracking-widest uppercase">
                        {step.label}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              {/* Items */}
              <div className="border-t border-border pt-6">
                <h3 className="text-xl font-bold uppercase">Items</h3>
                <div className="mt-4 divide-y divide-border">
                  {activeOrder.items.map((item, i) => (
                    <div key={i} className="flex justify-between py-3 text-sm">
                      <div>
                        <span className="font-bold uppercase">{item.name}</span>
                        <span className="text-muted-foreground ml-2 text-xs uppercase">
                          ({item.size} × {item.qty})
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <span className="font-bold">
                        {formatINR(item.price * item.qty)}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function TrackPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense
      fallback={
        <div className="p-12 text-center text-sm uppercase">Loading…</div>
      }
    >
      <TrackContent />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

================
File: src/auth.ts
================
import NextAuth from "next-auth";
import Google from "next-auth/providers/google";
import Credentials from "next-auth/providers/credentials";
import { MongoDBAdapter } from "@auth/mongodb-adapter";
import clientPromise from "@/lib/mongodb";
import bcrypt from "bcryptjs";

export const { handlers, signIn, signOut, auth } = NextAuth({
  adapter: MongoDBAdapter(clientPromise),
  session: { strategy: "jwt" },
  pages: {
    signIn: "/account/login",
  },
  providers: [
    Google({
      clientId: process.env.AUTH_GOOGLE_ID!,
      clientSecret: process.env.AUTH_GOOGLE_SECRET!,
      allowDangerousEmailAccountLinking: true,
    }),
    Credentials({
      name: "Credentials",
      credentials: {
        email: { label: "Email", type: "email" },
        password: { label: "Password", type: "password" },
      },
      async authorize(credentials) {
        if (!credentials?.email || !credentials?.password) return null;

        const client = await clientPromise;
        const db = client.db();
        const user = await db.collection("users").findOne({
          email: (credentials.email as string).toLowerCase().trim(),
        });

        if (!user || !user.password) return null;

        const isValid = await bcrypt.compare(
          credentials.password as string,
          user.password,
        );

        if (!isValid) return null;

        return {
          id: user._id.toString(),
          name: user.name,
          email: user.email,
        };
      },
    }),
  ],
  callbacks: {
    async jwt({ token, user }) {
      if (user) {
        token.id = user.id;
      }
      return token;
    },
    async session({ session, token }) {
      if (token && session.user) {
        session.user.id = token.id as string;
      }
      return session;
    },
  },
});

================
File: src/components/Account/SignOutButton.tsx
================
"use client";

import { signOut } from "next-auth/react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { LogOut } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";

export function SignOutButton() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleSignOut = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      await signOut({ redirect: false, callbackUrl: "/" });
      router.push("/");
      router.refresh();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Sign out error:", error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={handleSignOut}
      disabled={loading}
      className="cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-destructive hover:underline disabled:opacity-50"
    >
      <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
      {loading ? "Signing Out..." : "Sign Out"}
    </button>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { Minus, Plus, Trash2, X } from "lucide-react";
import { formatINR } from "@/data/products";
import { cartSubtotal, useCart } from "@/lib/store/cart";
import { useHydrated } from "@/hooks/use-hydrated";

const FREE_SHIPPING = 1499;

export function CartDrawer() {
  const { items, isOpen, close, setQty, removeItem } = useCart();
  const hydrated = useHydrated();
  const subtotal = cartSubtotal(items);
  const remaining = Math.max(0, FREE_SHIPPING - subtotal);

  if (!hydrated) return null;

  return (
    <>
      <div
        onClick={close}
        className={`fixed inset-0 z-[60] bg-black/60 transition-opacity duration-300 ${
          isOpen ? "opacity-100" : "pointer-events-none opacity-0"
        }`}
      />
      <aside
        className={`bg-background fixed top-0 right-0 z-[70] flex h-full w-full max-w-[420px] flex-col transition-transform duration-300 ${
          isOpen ? "translate-x-0" : "translate-x-full"
        }`}
      >
        <div className="bg-ink text-background flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-4">
          <h2 className="text-2xl tracking-[0.1em]">Your Bag</h2>
          <button
            onClick={close}
            aria-label="Close cart"
            className="hover:text-brand p-1 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X className="size-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-tile px-5 py-2.5 text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-[0.15em] uppercase">
          {remaining > 0
            ? `${formatINR(remaining)} away from free shipping`
            : "Free shipping unlocked"}
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
          {items.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex h-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-4 px-6 text-center">
              <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm tracking-wide uppercase">
                Your bag is empty
              </p>
              <Link
                href="/parfums"
                onClick={close}
                className="bg-ink text-background hover:bg-brand px-6 py-3 text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase transition-colors"
              >
                Shop parfums
              </Link>
            </div>
          ) : (
            items.map((item) => {
              const itemImg =
                typeof item.image === "string" ? item.image : item.image.src;
              return (
                <div
                  key={item.key}
                  className="border-border flex gap-4 border-b p-4"
                >
                  <img
                    src={itemImg}
                    alt={item.name}
                    width={1008}
                    height={1200}
                    loading="lazy"
                    className="bg-tile size-24 object-cover"
                  />
                  <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col">
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                      <h3 className="text-lg leading-tight">{item.name}</h3>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => removeItem(item.key)}
                        aria-label="Remove item"
                        className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-brand cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        <Trash2 className="size-4" />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-muted-foreground text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-[0.2em] uppercase">
                      {item.size}
                    </p>
                    <div className="mt-auto flex items-center justify-between">
                      <div className="border-border flex items-center border">
                        <button
                          onClick={() => setQty(item.key, item.qty - 1)}
                          aria-label="Decrease quantity"
                          className="hover:bg-tile p-2 cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          <Minus className="size-3.5" />
                        </button>
                        <span className="w-8 text-center text-sm font-bold">
                          {item.qty}
                        </span>
                        <button
                          onClick={() => setQty(item.key, item.qty + 1)}
                          aria-label="Increase quantity"
                          className="hover:bg-tile p-2 cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          <Plus className="size-3.5" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                      <p className="font-bold">
                        {formatINR(item.price * item.qty)}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })
          )}
        </div>

        {items.length > 0 && (
          <div className="border-border border-t p-5">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase">
                Subtotal
              </span>
              <span className="text-2xl font-bold">{formatINR(subtotal)}</span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-1 text-[11px] tracking-wide uppercase">
              Taxes included. Shipping at checkout.
            </p>
            <Link
              href="/checkout"
              onClick={close}
              className="bg-brand text-brand-foreground mt-4 block w-full py-4 text-center text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase transition-transform hover:-translate-y-0.5"
            >
              Checkout
            </Link>
          </div>
        )}
      </aside>
    </>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import hero1 from "@/assets/hero-1.jpg";
import hero2 from "@/assets/hero-2.jpg";
import trialSet from "@/assets/trial-set.jpg";

const slides = [
  {
    image: hero1,
    eyebrow: "New Extrait",
    title: ["BLK", "OPIUM"],
    copy: "Charred oud, black leather, 12+ hour projection.",
    cta: "Shop the drop",
    href: "/parfums/blk-opium",
  },
  {
    image: hero2,
    eyebrow: "House Collection",
    title: ["SMELL", "LOUD"],
    copy: "Six signature scents. Zero quiet ones.",
    cta: "Browse parfums",
    href: "/parfums",
  },
  {
    image: trialSet,
    eyebrow: "Starter Kit",
    title: ["TRIAL", "SET 06"],
    copy: "Six 2ML vials at ₹999. Credited on your first full bottle.",
    cta: "Get the set",
    href: "/parfums/trial-set-06",
  },
] as const;

export function HeroSlider() {
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  const go = useCallback(
    (i: number) =>
      setIndex(((i % slides.length) + slides.length) % slides.length),
    [],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setInterval(() => setIndex((i) => (i + 1) % slides.length), 6000);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, []);

  const slide = slides[index]!;

  return (
    <section className="bg-ink relative h-[78vh] min-h-[520px] w-full overflow-hidden">
      {slides.map((s, i) => {
        const imgSrc = typeof s.image === "string" ? s.image : s.image.src;
        return (
          <div
            key={i}
            className={`absolute inset-0 transition-opacity duration-700 ${
              i === index ? "opacity-100" : "pointer-events-none opacity-0"
            }`}
          >
            <img
              src={imgSrc}
              alt=""
              width={1600}
              height={1008}
              loading={i === 0 ? "eager" : "lazy"}
              className="size-full object-cover"
            />
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-black/85 via-black/45 to-transparent" />
          </div>
        );
      })}

      <div className="relative mx-auto flex h-full w-full max-w-[1400px] flex-col justify-end px-4 pb-16 sm:px-6 sm:pb-20">
        <span className="bg-brand text-brand-foreground mb-4 w-fit px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[0.3em] uppercase">
          {slide.eyebrow}
        </span>
        <h1 className="text-background display text-[18vw] leading-[0.82] sm:text-[10rem] lg:text-[12rem]">
          {slide.title[0]}
          <br />
          <span className="text-brand">{slide.title[1]}</span>
        </h1>
        <p className="text-background/80 mt-5 max-w-md text-sm tracking-wide sm:text-base">
          {slide.copy}
        </p>

        <div className="mt-8 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4">
          <Link
            href={slide.href}
            className="bg-brand text-brand-foreground px-8 py-4 text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase transition-transform hover:-translate-y-0.5"
          >
            {slide.cta}
          </Link>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {slides.map((_, i) => (
              <button
                key={i}
                onClick={() => go(i)}
                aria-label={`Slide ${i + 1}`}
                className={`h-[3px] transition-all ${
                  i === index ? "bg-brand w-10" : "w-5 bg-white/40"
                }`}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { Menu, ShoppingBag, User, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useSession } from "next-auth/react";
import { cartCount, useCart } from "@/lib/store/cart";
import { useHydrated } from "@/hooks/use-hydrated";

const links = [
  { href: "/", label: "Home" },
  { href: "/parfums", label: "Parfums" },
  { href: "/track", label: "Track Order" },
  { href: "/checkout", label: "Checkout" },
] as const;

export function Navbar() {
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const items = useCart((s) => s.items);
  const openCart = useCart((s) => s.open);
  const hydrated = useHydrated();
  const count = hydrated ? cartCount(items) : 0;

  const { data: session, status } = useSession();

  // Strict check: user object must exist and status must be authenticated
  const isAuthenticated =
    hydrated && status === "authenticated" && Boolean(session?.user);

  // Dynamic link destination and label
  const accountHref = isAuthenticated ? "/account" : "/account/login";
  const accountLabel = isAuthenticated ? "Account" : "Login";

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-50 w-full bg-ink text-background">
      <div className="bg-brand text-brand-foreground overflow-hidden py-1.5">
        <div className="marquee-track whitespace-nowrap text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-[0.3em] uppercase">
          {Array.from({ length: 2 }).map((_, i) => (
            <span key={i} className="flex shrink-0">
              {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((__, j) => (
                <span key={j} className="px-6">
                  Free shipping over ₹1499 — Cash on delivery available
                </span>
              ))}
            </span>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 w-full max-w-[1400px] items-center justify-between px-4 sm:px-6">
        <button
          className="-ml-2 p-2 md:hidden cursor-pointer"
          onClick={() => setMobileOpen((v) => !v)}
          aria-label="Toggle menu"
        >
          {mobileOpen ? <X className="size-5" /> : <Menu className="size-5" />}
        </button>

        <Link
          href="/"
          className="display text-3xl tracking-[0.18em] sm:text-4xl cursor-pointer"
        >
          CONS<span className="text-brand">CENT</span>
        </Link>

        <nav className="hidden items-center gap-8 md:flex">
          {links.map((l) => (
            <Link
              key={l.href}
              href={l.href}
              className="group relative text-xs font-semibold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase hover:text-brand cursor-pointer"
            >
              {l.label}
              <span className="bg-brand absolute -bottom-1.5 left-0 h-[2px] w-0 transition-all duration-300 group-hover:w-full" />
            </Link>
          ))}
        </nav>

        {/* Right side actions: Account/Login + Cart Bag */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 sm:gap-2">
          <Link
            href={accountHref}
            aria-label={accountLabel}
            className="hover:bg-brand flex items-center gap-1.5 p-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            <User className="size-5" />
            <span className="hidden text-xs font-semibold tracking-[0.2em] uppercase sm:inline">
              {accountLabel}
            </span>
          </Link>

          <button
            onClick={openCart}
            aria-label="Open cart"
            className="hover:bg-brand relative -mr-2 flex items-center gap-2 p-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            <ShoppingBag className="size-5" />
            <span className="hidden text-xs font-semibold tracking-[0.2em] uppercase sm:inline">
              Bag
            </span>
            {count > 0 && (
              <span className="bg-brand text-brand-foreground absolute -top-0.5 -right-1 flex size-5 items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-bold">
                {count}
              </span>
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {mobileOpen && (
        <nav className="border-t border-white/10 md:hidden">
          {links.map((l) => (
            <Link
              key={l.href}
              href={l.href}
              onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
              className="block border-b border-white/10 px-5 py-4 text-sm font-semibold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase hover:text-brand cursor-pointer"
            >
              {l.label}
            </Link>
          ))}
          <Link
            href={accountHref}
            onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-white/10 px-5 py-4 text-sm font-semibold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase text-brand cursor-pointer"
          >
            <User className="size-4" />
            {accountLabel}
          </Link>
        </nav>
      )}
    </header>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { formatINR } from "@/data/products";
import { useCart } from "@/lib/store/cart";
import type { Product } from "@/types";

export function ProductCard({ product }: { product: Product }) {
  const addItem = useCart((s) => s.addItem);
  const entry = product.variants[product.variants.length - 1]!;

  const imgSrc =
    typeof product.image === "string" ? product.image : product.image.src;

  return (
    <div className="group border-border bg-card relative flex flex-col border">
      {product.badge && (
        <span className="bg-brand text-brand-foreground absolute top-0 left-0 z-10 px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold tracking-[0.2em] uppercase">
          {product.badge}
        </span>
      )}

      <Link
        href={`/parfums/${product.slug}`}
        className="bg-tile block overflow-hidden"
      >
        <img
          src={imgSrc}
          alt={product.name}
          width={1008}
          height={1200}
          loading="lazy"
          className="aspect-5/6 w-full object-cover transition-transform duration-500 group-hover:scale-105"
        />
      </Link>

      <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col p-4">
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-[0.25em] uppercase">
          {product.family}
        </p>
        <Link href={`/parfums/${product.slug}`}>
          <h3 className="mt-1.5 text-2xl">{product.name}</h3>
        </Link>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-1 line-clamp-1 text-sm">
          {product.tagline}
        </p>

        <div className="mt-4 flex items-end justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-[0.2em] uppercase">
              {entry.size}
            </p>
            <p className="text-lg font-bold">
              {formatINR(entry.price)}
              {entry.compareAt && (
                <span className="text-muted-foreground ml-2 text-xs line-through">
                  {formatINR(entry.compareAt)}
                </span>
              )}
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              addItem(product, entry);
              toast.success(`${product.name} added to bag`);
            }}
            aria-label={`Add ${product.name} to bag`}
            className="bg-ink text-background hover:bg-brand flex size-11 items-center justify-center transition-colors"
          >
            <Plus className="size-5" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

  extends
    React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>,

import * as React from "react";
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md text-sm font-medium cursor-pointer transition-colors focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-1 focus-visible:ring-ring disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:size-4 [&_svg]:shrink-0",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-primary text-primary-foreground shadow hover:bg-primary/90",
        destructive: "bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground shadow-sm hover:bg-destructive/90",
        outline:
          "border border-input bg-background shadow-sm hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground",
        secondary: "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground shadow-sm hover:bg-secondary/80",
        ghost: "hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground",
        link: "text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
      },
      size: {
        default: "h-9 px-4 py-2",
        sm: "h-8 rounded-md px-3 text-xs",
        lg: "h-10 rounded-md px-8",
        icon: "h-9 w-9",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },
  },
);

export interface ButtonProps
  extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>, VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants> {
  asChild?: boolean;
}

const Button = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ className, variant, size, asChild = false, ...props }, ref) => {
    const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "button";
    return (
      <Comp className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, className }))} ref={ref} {...props} />
    );
  },
);
Button.displayName = "Button";

export { Button, buttonVariants };

================
File: src/components/ui/carousel.tsx
================
import * as React from "react";
import useEmblaCarousel, { type UseEmblaCarouselType } from "embla-carousel-react";
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

type CarouselApi = UseEmblaCarouselType[1];
type UseCarouselParameters = Parameters<typeof useEmblaCarousel>;
type CarouselOptions = UseCarouselParameters[0];
type CarouselPlugin = UseCarouselParameters[1];

type CarouselProps = {
  opts?: CarouselOptions;
  plugins?: CarouselPlugin;
  orientation?: "horizontal" | "vertical";
  setApi?: (api: CarouselApi) => void;
};

type CarouselContextProps = {
  carouselRef: ReturnType<typeof useEmblaCarousel>[0];
  api: ReturnType<typeof useEmblaCarousel>[1];
  scrollPrev: () => void;
  scrollNext: () => void;
  canScrollPrev: boolean;
  canScrollNext: boolean;
} & CarouselProps;

const CarouselContext = React.createContext<CarouselContextProps | null>(null);

function useCarousel() {
  const context = React.useContext(CarouselContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useCarousel must be used within a <Carousel />");
  }

  return context;
}

const Carousel = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLDivElement,
  React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> & CarouselProps
>(({ orientation = "horizontal", opts, setApi, plugins, className, children, ...props }, ref) => {
  const [carouselRef, api] = useEmblaCarousel(
    {
      ...opts,
      axis: orientation === "horizontal" ? "x" : "y",
    },
    plugins,
  );
  const [canScrollPrev, setCanScrollPrev] = React.useState(false);
  const [canScrollNext, setCanScrollNext] = React.useState(false);

  const onSelect = React.useCallback((api: CarouselApi) => {
    if (!api) {
      return;
    }

    setCanScrollPrev(api.canScrollPrev());
    setCanScrollNext(api.canScrollNext());
  }, []);

  const scrollPrev = React.useCallback(() => {
    api?.scrollPrev();
  }, [api]);

  const scrollNext = React.useCallback(() => {
    api?.scrollNext();
  }, [api]);

  const handleKeyDown = React.useCallback(
    (event: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
      if (event.key === "ArrowLeft") {
        event.preventDefault();
        scrollPrev();
      } else if (event.key === "ArrowRight") {
        event.preventDefault();
        scrollNext();
      }
    },
    [scrollPrev, scrollNext],
  );

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!api || !setApi) {
      return;
    }

    setApi(api);
  }, [api, setApi]);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!api) {
      return;
    }

    onSelect(api);
    api.on("reInit", onSelect);
    api.on("select", onSelect);

    return () => {
      api?.off("select", onSelect);
    };
  }, [api, onSelect]);

  return (
    <CarouselContext.Provider
      value={{
        carouselRef,
        api: api,
        opts,
        orientation: orientation || (opts?.axis === "y" ? "vertical" : "horizontal"),
        scrollPrev,
        scrollNext,
        canScrollPrev,
        canScrollNext,
      }}
    >
      <div
        ref={ref}
        onKeyDownCapture={handleKeyDown}
        className={cn("relative", className)}
        role="region"
        aria-roledescription="carousel"
        {...props}
      >
        {children}
      </div>
    </CarouselContext.Provider>
  );
});
Carousel.displayName = "Carousel";

const CarouselContent = React.forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>>(
  ({ className, ...props }, ref) => {
    const { carouselRef, orientation } = useCarousel();

    return (
      <div ref={carouselRef} className="overflow-hidden">
        <div
          ref={ref}
          className={cn(
            "flex",
            orientation === "horizontal" ? "-ml-4" : "-mt-4 flex-col",
            className,
          )}
          {...props}
        />
      </div>
    );
  },
);
CarouselContent.displayName = "CarouselContent";

const CarouselItem = React.forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>>(
  ({ className, ...props }, ref) => {
    const { orientation } = useCarousel();

    return (
      <div
        ref={ref}
        role="group"
        aria-roledescription="slide"
        className={cn(
          "min-w-0 shrink-0 grow-0 basis-full",
          orientation === "horizontal" ? "pl-4" : "pt-4",
          className,
        )}
        {...props}
      />
    );
  },
);
CarouselItem.displayName = "CarouselItem";

const CarouselPrevious = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, React.ComponentProps<typeof Button>>(
  ({ className, variant = "outline", size = "icon", ...props }, ref) => {
    const { orientation, scrollPrev, canScrollPrev } = useCarousel();

    return (
      <Button
        ref={ref}
        variant={variant}
        size={size}
        className={cn(
          "absolute  h-8 w-8 rounded-full",
          orientation === "horizontal"
            ? "-left-12 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2"
            : "-top-12 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 rotate-90",
          className,
        )}
        disabled={!canScrollPrev}
        onClick={scrollPrev}
        {...props}
      >
        <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
        <span className="sr-only">Previous slide</span>
      </Button>
    );
  },
);
CarouselPrevious.displayName = "CarouselPrevious";

const CarouselNext = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, React.ComponentProps<typeof Button>>(
  ({ className, variant = "outline", size = "icon", ...props }, ref) => {
    const { orientation, scrollNext, canScrollNext } = useCarousel();

    return (
      <Button
        ref={ref}
        variant={variant}
        size={size}
        className={cn(
          "absolute h-8 w-8 rounded-full",
          orientation === "horizontal"
            ? "-right-12 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2"
            : "-bottom-12 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 rotate-90",
          className,
        )}
        disabled={!canScrollNext}
        onClick={scrollNext}
        {...props}
      >
        <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
        <span className="sr-only">Next slide</span>
      </Button>
    );
  },
);
CarouselNext.displayName = "CarouselNext";

export {
  type CarouselApi,
  Carousel,
  CarouselContent,
  CarouselItem,
  CarouselPrevious,
  CarouselNext,
};

"use client";

import * as React from "react";

  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof DialogPrimitive.Overlay>

      "fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/80  data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=open]:fade-in-0",

    )}
    {...props}
  />

File: src/components/ui/form.tsx
================
import * as React from "react";
import * as LabelPrimitive from "@radix-ui/react-label";
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot";
import {
  Controller,
  FormProvider,
  useFormContext,
  type ControllerProps,
  type FieldPath,
  type FieldValues,
} from "react-hook-form";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";

const Form = FormProvider;

type FormFieldContextValue<
  TFieldValues extends FieldValues = FieldValues,
  TName extends FieldPath<TFieldValues> = FieldPath<TFieldValues>,
> = {
  name: TName;
};

const FormFieldContext = React.createContext<FormFieldContextValue | null>(null);

const FormField = <
  TFieldValues extends FieldValues = FieldValues,
  TName extends FieldPath<TFieldValues> = FieldPath<TFieldValues>,
>({
  ...props
}: ControllerProps<TFieldValues, TName>) => {
  return (
    <FormFieldContext.Provider value={{ name: props.name }}>
      <Controller {...props} />
    </FormFieldContext.Provider>
  );
};

const useFormField = () => {
  const fieldContext = React.useContext(FormFieldContext);
  const itemContext = React.useContext(FormItemContext);
  const { getFieldState, formState } = useFormContext();

  if (!fieldContext) {
    throw new Error("useFormField should be used within <FormField>");
  }

  if (!itemContext) {
    throw new Error("useFormField should be used within <FormItem>");
  }

  const fieldState = getFieldState(fieldContext.name, formState);

  const { id } = itemContext;

  return {
    id,
    name: fieldContext.name,
    formItemId: `${id}-form-item`,
    formDescriptionId: `${id}-form-item-description`,
    formMessageId: `${id}-form-item-message`,
    ...fieldState,
  };
};

type FormItemContextValue = {
  id: string;
};

const FormItemContext = React.createContext<FormItemContextValue | null>(null);

const FormItem = React.forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>>(
  ({ className, ...props }, ref) => {
    const id = React.useId();

    return (
      <FormItemContext.Provider value={{ id }}>
        <div ref={ref} className={cn("space-y-2", className)} {...props} />
      </FormItemContext.Provider>
    );
  },
);
FormItem.displayName = "FormItem";

const FormLabel = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof LabelPrimitive.Root>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof LabelPrimitive.Root>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => {
  const { error, formItemId } = useFormField();

  return (
    <Label
      ref={ref}
      className={cn(error && "text-destructive", className)}
      htmlFor={formItemId}
      {...props}
    />
  );
});
FormLabel.displayName = "FormLabel";

const FormControl = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof Slot>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof Slot>
>(({ ...props }, ref) => {
  const { error, formItemId, formDescriptionId, formMessageId } = useFormField();

  return (
    <Slot
      ref={ref}
      id={formItemId}
      aria-describedby={!error ? `${formDescriptionId}` : `${formDescriptionId} ${formMessageId}`}
      aria-invalid={!!error}
      {...props}
    />
  );
});
FormControl.displayName = "FormControl";

const FormDescription = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLParagraphElement,
  React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLParagraphElement>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => {
  const { formDescriptionId } = useFormField();

  return (
    <p
      ref={ref}
      id={formDescriptionId}
      className={cn("text-[0.8rem] text-muted-foreground", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  );
});
FormDescription.displayName = "FormDescription";

const FormMessage = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLParagraphElement,
  React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLParagraphElement>
>(({ className, children, ...props }, ref) => {
  const { error, formMessageId } = useFormField();
  const body = error ? String(error?.message ?? "") : children;

  if (!body) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <p
      ref={ref}
      id={formMessageId}
      className={cn("text-[0.8rem] font-medium text-destructive", className)}
      {...props}
    >
      {body}
    </p>
  );
});
FormMessage.displayName = "FormMessage";

export {
  useFormField,
  Form,
  FormItem,
  FormLabel,
  FormControl,
  FormDescription,
  FormMessage,
  FormField,
};
